&\times\Bigg[f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\, U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{d}(x)- f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{acd}\,U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{e}(x) \notag \\ & -f^{ac^{\prime}d^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{c^{\prime}b}(z)\,U^{d^{\prime}e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{d}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{de^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg]\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2e}}{d^{3}k}&= -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-w^{\prime})}}\,\frac{1}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\, U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{d}(x)- f^{c^{\prime}bd^{\prime}}\,f^{acd}\,U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rho^{e}(x) \notag \\ & -f^{ac^{\prime}d^{\prime}}\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{c^{\prime}b}(z)\,U^{d^{\prime}e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{d}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{de^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg]\notag
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row II}
\textsc{NA}
\subsubsection*{Row III}
\textsc{NA}
\subsubsection*{Row IV}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.IV}}{d^{3}k}= &  +\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}\,N_{c}}{4\pi^{3}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\vee-k^{+}}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\,\frac{p^{+}}{(p^{+}+k^{+})^{2}}\int_{x,y,x',y'}e^{-ik\cdot{(y-y')}}\,e^{-ik\cdot{(y-y')}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\Big[\mathcal{K}(x-y)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\notag \\ & -2\,\mathcal{K}(x-y)\cdot(y'-y)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-y)\Big]\times\Big[U^{ce'}(y') - U^{ce'}(x')\Big]\Big[U^{ce}(y) - U^{ce}(x)\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\rho^{e}(x)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}\,N_{c}}{2\pi^{3}}\,\frac{1}{k^{2}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,x',z}\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\Big[U^{ce'}(z)-U^{ce'}(x')\Big]\Big[U^{ce}(z)-U^{ce}(x)\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\rho^{e}(x)
\label{eq:D-II.IVrem}
\end{align}
\subsection{Group III}
\subsubsection*{Row I}
\textsc{NA} -- Cancels with Group I, Row IV
\subsubsection*{Row II}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.II}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{3\rho}&=-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,x',z,w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-w')}}\,\bigg\{\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\}{\color{red}\Big\{}U^{ma}(y)\,U^{gb}(x')\notag \\ & U^{mh}(z)\,\Big[f^{khc}\,U^{gk}(w)-f^{gkl}\,U^{kh}(z)\,U^{lc}(x)\Big]-U^{gb}(x')\Big[f^{kac}\,U^{gk}(w)-f^{gkl}\,U^{ka}(z)\,U^{lc}(x)\Big]{\color{red}\Big\}}\,\big\{\rho^{a}(y)\rho^{b}(x')\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-III.II-3}
\end{align}
Two-$\rho$ terms after symmetrization:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.II}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{2\rho}&=-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,x',z,w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-w')}}\,\bigg\{\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\}\times\,\notag\\
& \frac{i}{2}\,{\color{red}\Big\{}U^{ma}(y)\,U^{gb}(x')\,U^{mh}(z)\Big[f^{khc}\,U^{gk}(w)-f^{gkl}\,U^{kh}(z)\,U^{lc}(x)\Big]-U^{gb}(x')\Big[f^{kac}\,U^{gk}(w)-f^{gkl}\,U^{ka}(z)\,U^{lc}(x)\Big]{\color{red}\Big\}}\notag \\ & \Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x')\big\{\rho^{r}(y)\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym} +f^{bcr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{a}(y)\rho^{r}(x')\big\}_{\rm sym} +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(y)\rho^{b}(x')\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-III.II-2}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row III}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.III}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{3\rho}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,x',y',z,y,x}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-y')}}e^{-ik\cdot{(z-y')}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{1}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&{-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times 2\,f^{mnk}\Big[U^{ma}(y')-U^{ma}(x')\Big]\Big[U^{nc}(z)\,U^{kb}(w)-U^{kb}(y)\,U^{nc}(x)\Big]\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times 2\,f^{mnk}\Big[U^{ma}(z)-U^{ma}(x')\Big]\Big[U^{nc}(z)\,U^{kb}(w)-U^{kb}(y)\,U^{nc}(x)\Big]\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-III.III-3}
\end{align}
 Two-$\rho$ terms after symmetrisation:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.III}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{2\rho}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,x',y',z,y,x}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-y')}}e^{-ik\cdot{(z-y')}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{1}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&{-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}-\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times i\,f^{mnk}\Big[U^{ma}(y')-U^{ma}(x')\Big]\Big[U^{nc}(z)\,U^{kb}(w)-U^{kb}(y)\,U^{nc}(x)\Big]\notag \\ & \Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym} +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\times i\,f^{mnk}\Big[U^{ma}(z)-U^{ma}(x')\Big]\Big[U^{nc}(z)\,U^{kb}(w)-U^{kb}(y)\,U^{nc}(x)\Big]\notag \\ & \Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym} +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-III.III-2}
\end{align}
Genuine $2-\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.III}_{2}}{d^{3}k} & =\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,x',y',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-y')}}e^{-ik\cdot{(z-y')}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\frac{1}{p^{+}\,(x-z)^{2}+k^{+}\,(x-w)^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\Bigg\{\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg[\frac{p^{+}\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{(z-w)^{2}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})^{2}}\notag\\
&+\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big((x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)-\frac{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\Big)\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\Big((x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)+\frac{1}{2}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\frac{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\Big)\bigg]\notag\\
&+\frac{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}{(p^{+}+k^{+})\,(x-w)^{2}}\Big[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)+\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\Big]\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{k^{+}}\Big[(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\notag\\
&+(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\Big]\Bigg\}\notag\\
&\times\Big[f^{c'd'a}\,U^{c'e'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-f^{c'd'a}\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\Big]\notag\\
&\times\Big[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x)-f^{acd}\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\Big]\notag\\
&{-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big]_{+}\int_{w,x',y',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-y')}}e^{-ik\cdot{(z-y')}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\frac{1}{(x-w)^{2}}}\notag\\
&{\times\Big[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)+\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\Big]}\notag\\
&\times\Big[f^{c'd'a}\,U^{c'e'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-f^{c'd'a}\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\Big]\notag\\
&\times\Big[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x)-f^{acd}\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\Big]\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+\,2}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,x',y',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-y')}}e^{-ik\cdot{(z-y')}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\frac{p^{+}}{p^{+}\,(x-z)^{2}+k^{+}\,(x-w)^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\Big[(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\Big]\notag\\
&\times\Big[f^{c'd'a}\,U^{c'e'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-f^{c'd'a}\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\Big]\notag\\
&-\Big[(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\Big]\notag\\
&\times\Big[f^{c'd'a}\,U^{c'e'}(z)\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-f^{c'd'a}\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\Big]\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times\Big[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x)-f^{acd}\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\Big]\notag\\
& +\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,x',w',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-w')}}\,\frac{(x-w)^{2}}{\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\right]\notag \\ &  \bigg[\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)-\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)\bigg]   \times\notag \\ & \left[f^{c'd'a}\,U^{c'e'}(z)\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-f^{c'd'a}\, U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\right]\notag\\ &\times\left[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x) -f^{acd}\,U^{cb}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\right]\,+{\rm c.c.}
\label{eq:D-III.III-2o}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row IV}
\textsc{NA}
\subsubsection*{Row V}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.V}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{3\rho}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}\,f^{mkl}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-y')}}e^{-ik\cdot{(z-y')}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{1}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&{-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \Big[U^{ma}(y')-U^{ma}(x')\Big]U^{kb}(x)\Big[U^{lc}(y)-U^{lc}(w)\Big]\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \Big[U^{ma}(z)-U^{ma}(x')\Big]U^{kb}(x)\Big[U^{lc}(y)-U^{lc}(w)\Big]\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-III.V-3}
\end{align}
Two-$\rho$ terms from symmetrization: 
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.V}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{2\rho}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}\,f^{mkl}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-y')}}e^{-ik\cdot{(z-y')}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{1}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&{-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}-\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{i}{2}\,\Big[U^{ma}(y')-U^{ma}(x')\Big]U^{kb}(x)\Big[U^{lc}(y)-U^{lc}(w)\Big]\Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
& +f^{bcr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y)\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{r}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}+f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\times \frac{i}{2}\,\Big[U^{ma}(z)-U^{ma}(x')\Big]U^{kb}(x)\Big[U^{lc}(y)-U^{lc}(w)\Big]\times\,\notag \\ & \Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym} +f^{bcr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y)\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{r}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}+f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-III.V-2}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row VI}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.VI}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{3\rho}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}\,f^{mnk}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-y')}}e^{-ik\cdot{(z-y')}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{1}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&{-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \Big[U^{ma}(y')-U^{ma}(x')\Big]U^{kb}(y)\Big[U^{nc}(x)-U^{nc}(z)\Big]\,\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \Big[U^{ma}(z)-U^{ma}(x')\Big]U^{kb}(y)\Big[U^{nc}(x)-U^{nc}(z)\Big]\,\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-III.VI-3}
\end{align}
Two-$\rho$ terms after symmetrisation:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.VI}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{2\rho}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}\,f^{mnk}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-y')}}e^{-ik\cdot{(z-y')}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{1}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&{-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{i}{2}\,\Big[U^{ma}(y')-U^{ma}(x')\Big]U^{kb}(y)\Big[U^{nc}(x)-U^{nc}(z)\Big]\times\,\Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
& +f^{bcr}\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{r}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym} +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{i}{2}\,\Big[U^{ma}(z)-U^{ma}(x')\Big]U^{kb}(y)\Big[U^{nc}(x)-U^{nc}(z)\Big]\times\,\Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
& +f^{bcr}\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{r}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym} +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:D-III.VI-2}
\end{align}
\section{Average over the projectile}\label{sec:E}
Using
\begin{align}
\big\langle\{\rho^{a}(x)\rho^{b}(y)\}_{\rm sym}\big\rangle=\big\langle\rho^{a}(x)\rho^{b}(y)\big\rangle_{x\neq y}=\delta^{ab}\,\frac{\langle\rho^{f}(x)\rho^{f}(y)\big\rangle}{N_{c}^{2}-1}
\end{align}
the symmetrised three-point function is,
\begin{align}
\big\langle\{\rho^{a}(x)\rho^{b}(y)\rho^{c}(z)\}_{\rm sym}\big\rangle=d^{abc}\,\frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,{\color{red}\big\langle}d^{efg}\{\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{f}(y)\rho^{g}(z)\}_{\rm sym}\big\rangle
\end{align}
We will denote the following: 
$$n(x,y) = \langle\rho^{f}(x)\rho^{f}(y)\big\rangle$$
$$\sigma(x,y,z) = {\color{red}\big\langle}d^{efg}\{\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{f}(y)\rho^{g}(z)\}_{\rm sym}\big\rangle$$
\fixnote{(i) The opening $\langle$ was missing in the second formula and in the definition of $\sigma$. (ii) $n(x,y)=n(y,x)$ is the two-point function at $x\neq y$ (the contact parts are in the two-$\rho$ rows); $\sigma$ is real and totally symmetric; $\langle\rho^{a}(x)\rangle=0$; the $f^{abc}$ part of the three-point function vanishes; for $SU(2)$, $d^{abc}=0$ and the $\sigma$ terms are absent. (iii) With these, for the colour tensor $C^{abc}$ of a row (one-$\rho$ terms average to zero):
$\langle C^{abc}\rho^{a}(p_{1})\rho^{b}(p_{2})\rho^{c}(p_{3})\rangle=\frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}C^{abc}d^{abc}\,\sigma(p_{1},p_{2},p_{3})+\frac{i}{2(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\big[\Phi_{12}\delta^{(2)}(p_{1}-p_{2})n(p_{1},p_{3})+\Phi_{23}\delta^{(2)}(p_{2}-p_{3})n(p_{1},p_{2})-\Phi_{13}\delta^{(2)}(p_{1}-p_{3})n(p_{1},p_{2})\big]$, $\Phi_{ij}=C^{abc}f^{abc}$ with the Wilson lines at $p_{i}=p_{j}$; for the anticommutator types the factor $2$ on the first term and $i[\Phi_{12}\delta_{12}n-\Phi_{13}\delta_{13}n]$, resp.\ $i[\Phi_{23}\delta_{23}n-\Phi_{13}\delta_{13}n]$. (iv) The equations below wrote $o(\dots)$ for $\sigma(\dots)$; replaced (red). (v) In the contact brackets a red $+$ is inserted between the terms. (vi) All colour factors of this section were checked numerically (summary at the top).}
\subsection{Group I}
\subsubsection*{Row I}
\textsc{NA}
\subsubsection*{Row II}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ part:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.II}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(3\rho)}&=-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{64\pi^{6}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\int_{x,y,x'}\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\int d^{2}p\,d^{2}q\;e^{iq\cdot(w-y)}\,e^{ip\cdot(y-x)}\,\frac{(x'-w')^{i}}{(x'-w')^{2}\,\mathbf{q}^{2}}\,\frac{1}{2}\bigg[\frac{R^{i}(\mathbf{q},\mathbf{p})}{\mathbf{p}^{2}}-\frac{R^{i}(\mathbf{q},\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})}{(\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})^{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(+2i\Big)\,{\color{red}\sigma}(x',x,y)\Big[{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w')U(x)\}\Big]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-I.II-3}
\end{align}
where: $$R^{i}(\mathbf{q},\mathbf{p})  = -2q^{i}\log\left(\frac{\mathbf{p}^{2}}{\mathbf{q}^{2}}\right)+\frac{(2\mathbf{p}^{2}-2\,\mathbf{q}\cdot\mathbf{p}+\mathbf{q}^{2})\,q^{i}+\mathbf{q}^{2}p^{i}}{(\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})^{2}}\,\log\left[\frac{\mathbf{q}^{2}}{(\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})^{2}}\right]$$
Two $\rho$ terms after symmetrisation
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.II}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(2\rho)}&=-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{64\pi^{6}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\int_{x,y,x'}\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\int d^{2}p\,d^{2}q\;e^{iq\cdot(w-y)}\,e^{ip\cdot(y-x)}\,\frac{(x'-w')^{i}}{(x'-w')^{2}\,\mathbf{q}^{2}}\,\frac{1}{2}\bigg[\frac{R^{i}(\mathbf{q},\mathbf{p})}{\mathbf{p}^{2}}-\frac{R^{i}(\mathbf{q},\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})}{(\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})^{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)\}\notag\\
& -N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w')\}\Big]\notag\\
& {\color{red}+}\Big(-i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(y)U(w)\}\notag\\
& -N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w')\}\Big]\bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-I.II-2}
\end{align}
Genuine two-$\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.II}_{1}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(2\rho)} & = +\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}\,N_{c}}{32\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\Big(-\frac{67}{9}+\frac{\pi^{2}}{3}\Big)\int_{w,w',x,x'} e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\;\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\notag \\
& \times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x')U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}-{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w')U(w)\}+{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w')U(x)\}\Big]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-I.II-2o}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row III}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ part:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.III}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(3\rho)}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\int_{x,y,z,x'}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\notag\\
&+\frac{\arccos\left(\frac{(x-w)^{2}-(x-w)\cdot (x-z)}{\sqrt{(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w)^{2}}}\right)}{\sqrt{(x-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-\left[(x-w)\cdot (x-z)\right]^{2}}\,(z-w)^{2}}\,\bigg[\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(x-w)\notag\\
&-\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-z)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(x-z)\notag\\
&+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)-\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)\bigg]\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&+\bigg[-\frac{1}{2}\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}-\int_{0}^{k^{+}}dp^{+}\Big[\frac{1}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot(z-w)\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\bigg]\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\notag\\
&+\int_{0}^{k^{+}}dp^{+}\Big[\frac{1}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot(z-w)\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(-i\Big)\,{\color{red}\sigma}(x',x,y)\Big[{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w')U(x)\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(x)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w')U(x)\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{-\int_{0}^{k^{+}}dp^{+}\Big[\frac{1}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot(z-w)\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(-i\Big)\,{\color{red}\sigma}(x',x,y)\Big[{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w')U(x)\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w)U(x)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w')U(x)\}\Big]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-I.III-3}
\end{align}
Two $\rho$ term after symmetrization
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.III}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(2\rho)}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\int_{x,y,z,x'}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\notag\\
&+\frac{\arccos\left(\frac{(x-w)^{2}-(x-w)\cdot (x-z)}{\sqrt{(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w)^{2}}}\right)}{\sqrt{(x-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-\left[(x-w)\cdot (x-z)\right]^{2}}\,(z-w)^{2}}\,\bigg[\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(x-w)\notag\\
&-\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-z)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(x-z)\notag\\
&+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)-\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)\bigg]\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&+\bigg[-\frac{1}{2}\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}-\int_{0}^{k^{+}}dp^{+}\Big[\frac{1}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot(z-w)\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\bigg]\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\notag\\
&+\int_{0}^{k^{+}}dp^{+}\Big[\frac{1}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot(z-w)\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w')\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}\Big]\notag\\
& {\color{red}+}\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(w')\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}\Big]\notag\\
& {\color{red}+}\Big(-\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w')\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}\Big]\bigg]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{-\int_{0}^{k^{+}}dp^{+}\Big[\frac{1}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot(z-w)\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w')\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\Big]\notag\\
& {\color{red}+}\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(w')\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(w)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\Big]\notag\\
& {\color{red}+}\Big(-\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w)\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w')\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\Big]\bigg]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-I.III-2}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row IV}
\textsc{NA} - canceled with Group III, Row I
\subsubsection*{Row V}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ part:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.V}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(3\rho)}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+\,2}}\int_{0}^{k^{+}}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{x,z,y,y',x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot(z-w')}\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{e^{-ik\cdot(x-z)\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\notag\\
&-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot(x-z)\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\notag\\
&-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot(x-z)}e^{-ik\cdot(z-x)\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(+2i\Big)\,{\color{red}\sigma}(x',y,y')\Big[{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(x)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w')U(x)\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(y)U(x')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(y')U(y)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w')U(y)\}\Big]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-I.V-3}
\end{align}
Two $\rho$ term after symmetrization
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.V}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(2\rho)} &=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+\,2}}\int_{0}^{k^{+}}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{x,z,y,y',x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot(z-w')}\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{e^{-ik\cdot(x-z)\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\notag\\
&-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot(x-z)\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\notag\\
&-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot(x-z)}e^{-ik\cdot(z-x)\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,y')\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(w')\}\Big]\notag\\
& {\color{red}+}\Big(-i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y')\,n(y',y)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(z)\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(w')\}\Big]\bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-I.V-2}
\end{align}
Genuine two-$\rho$ terms:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.V}_{2}}{d^{3}k} & =\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+\,2}}\int_{0}^{k^{+}}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{x,y,z,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot{(z-w')}}\notag\\
&\times\Bigg\{\frac{k^{+}\,e^{-ik\cdot{(x-z)}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}}{p^{+}\,(y-x)^{2}+(k^{+}-p^{+})\,(y-z)^{2}}\bigg[\frac{p^{+}(k^{+}-p^{+})}{k^{+\,2}}\,\frac{(y-x)^{2}-(y-z)^{2}}{(x-z)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\notag\\
&+\frac{k^{+}-p^{+}}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\Big[(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)+\frac{1}{2}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)-\frac{(y-x)^{2}-(y-z)^{2}}{2\,(x-z)^{2}}\Big]\notag\\
&+\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\Big[(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\frac{1}{2}(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)-\frac{(y-x)^{2}-(y-z)^{2}}{2\,(x-z)^{2}}\Big]\notag\\
&+\frac{(y-x)^{2}}{(y-z)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\,(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)\Big]\notag\\
&+\frac{(y-z)^{2}}{(y-x)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}-\frac{1}{2}\,(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\Big]\notag\\
&-(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')+\frac{1}{2}\,(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\notag\\
&-(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')-\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\bigg]\notag\\
&{-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}\frac{e^{-ik\cdot{(x-z)}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}}{(y-z)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\,(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)\Big]}\notag\\
&{-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{k^{+}-p^{+}}\Big]_{+}\frac{e^{-ik\cdot{(x-z)}\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}}{(y-x)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}-\frac{1}{2}\,(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\Big]}\Bigg\}\notag\\
&\times\frac{n(x',y)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(w')\}\notag\\ &-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}\notag\\
&+N_{c}\Big(-{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(w')\}+{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(y)U(x')\}-{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(y)U(w')\}\Big)\Big]\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}N_{c}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot(z-w')}\,e^{-ik\cdot(x-z)}\,\frac{11}{6}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)\,\frac{(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{(x-z)^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\frac{n(x',y)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[-{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(w')\}+{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(y)U(x')\}-{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(y)U(w')\}\Big]\,+{\rm c.c.}\,,\label{eq:E-I.V-2o}
\end{align}
\subsection{Group II}
\subsubsection*{Row I}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ part:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(3\rho)}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,w,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{-\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(-2i\Big)\,{\color{red}\sigma}(x',y,x)\Big[{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w')U(x)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(w)\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w')^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w')\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w')^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(-2i\Big)\,{\color{red}\sigma}(x,x',y)\Big[{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w)U(x)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(w')\}\Big]\Bigg]\label{eq:E-II.I-3}
\end{align}
Two $\rho$ term after symmetrization:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(2\rho)} &=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,w,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{-\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(w)U(x')\}\Big]\notag\\
& {\color{red}+}\Big(-i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(y)\}\Big]\bigg]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w')^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w')\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w')^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(w')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(y)\}\Big]\notag\\
& {\color{red}+}\Big(-i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(w')U(x')\}\Big]\bigg]\Bigg]\label{eq:E-II.I-2}
\end{align}
Genuine two-$\rho$ terms:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2a}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{2\rho}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w^{\prime})}\,\frac{1}{\big[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\right]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\frac{1}{2}\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\notag \\ & +\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime}) -\frac{1}{4}\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\notag \\ & +(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})+\frac{1}{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{2}\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)-\frac{1}{4}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\Bigg]\notag\\ &\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Bigg[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\notag\\ &-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Bigg]\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2b}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(2\rho)}&= -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w^{\prime})}\,\frac{1}{\big[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-w)^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\right]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\frac{1}{2}\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\notag \\ & +\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime}) -\frac{1}{4}\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\notag \\ & +(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})+\frac{1}{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{2}\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)-\frac{1}{4}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\Bigg]\notag\\ &\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Bigg[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\notag\\ &-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Bigg]\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2c}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(2\rho)} &= \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w^{\prime})}\,\frac{1}{\big[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\right]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[-\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg[\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\,(z-w)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(z-w)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,(x-z)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})}{2\,(x-z)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)}{4\,(x-z)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\bigg]\notag\\
&-\frac{(x-w)^{2}}{(x-z)^{2}}\bigg[\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x-z)\,(z-w)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}-\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x-z)\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(z-w)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & -\frac{(x-w)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})\,(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)}{2\,(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)}{4\,(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\bigg]\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\{(x-z)^{2}\bigg[\frac{(x-w)\cdot(z-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{4\,(x-z)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag\\ &+\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(z-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\bigg]-(x-w)^{2}\bigg[\frac{(x-z)\cdot(z-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & -\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{4\,(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & -\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}-\frac{1}{2}\,\frac{(x-z)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}\Bigg]\notag\\ &\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Bigg[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\notag\\ &-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Bigg]\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2d}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(2\rho)}&= \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w^{\prime})}\,\frac{1}{\big[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\right]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\bigg[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})+\frac{1}{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\notag \\ & +\frac{1}{2}\,(x-w)\cdot(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})+\frac{1}{4}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\bigg]\notag\\
&+\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\bigg[(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})-\frac{1}{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)+\frac{1}{4}\,(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\bigg]\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}-(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\bigg\{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\bigg[\frac{(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag\\ &+\frac{(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w)^{2}}\bigg]\notag\\
&-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\bigg[\frac{(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}-\frac{(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}-\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w)^{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\,\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}\Bigg]\notag\\ &\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Bigg[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\notag\\ &-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Bigg]\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2e}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(2\rho)}&= -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w^{\prime})}\,\frac{1}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag\\ &\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Bigg[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(w)U(w')\}\notag\\ &-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Bigg]\notag
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row II}
\textsc{NA}
\subsubsection*{Row III}
\textsc{NA}
\subsubsection*{Row IV}

\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.IV}_{\rm fin}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(2\rho)}= &  +\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}\,N_{c}}{4\pi^{3}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\vee-k^{+}}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\,\frac{p^{+}}{(p^{+}+k^{+})^{2}}\int_{x,y,x',y'}e^{-ik\cdot(y-y')}\,e^{-ik\cdot(y-y')\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\Big[\mathcal{K}(x-y)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\notag \\ & -2\,\mathcal{K}(x-y)\cdot(y'-y)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-y)\Big]\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\,{\rm Tr}\Big\{\big(U^{\dagger}(y')-U^{\dagger}(x')\big)\big(U(y)-U(x)\big)\Big\}\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}\,N_{c}}{2\pi^{3}}\,\frac{1}{k^{2}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,x',z}\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\,{\rm Tr}\Big\{\big(U^{\dagger}(z)-U^{\dagger}(x')\big)\big(U(z)-U(x)\big)\Big\}\label{eq:E-II.IVrem}
\end{align}
\subsection{Group III}
\subsubsection*{Row I}
\textsc{NA} - canceled with Group I, Row IV
\subsubsection*{Row II}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ part:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.II}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(3\rho)}&=-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,x',z,w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(-i\Big)\,{\color{red}\sigma}(y,x',x)\Big[{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(x)\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(w)\}\Big]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-III.II-3}
\end{align}
Two $\rho$ term after symmetrization
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.II}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(2\rho)}&=-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,x',z,w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(y)U(w)\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\notag\\
& {\color{red}+}\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)\}\notag\\
& +N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(y)\}\Big]\notag\\
& {\color{red}+}\Big(-\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x')U(w)\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(w)U(x')\}\Big]\bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-III.II-2}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row III}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ part:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.III}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(3\rho)}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,x',y',z,y,x}e^{-ik\cdot(w-y')}e^{-ik\cdot(z-y')\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{1}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(-2i\Big)\,{\color{red}\sigma}(x',y,x)\Big[{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(y')U(x)\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w)U(x')\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(y')U(z)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w)U(y')\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(-2i\Big)\,{\color{red}\sigma}(x',y,x)\Big[{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(x)\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w)U(x')\}\Big]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-III.III-3}
\end{align}
Two $\rho$ term after symmetrization:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.III}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(2\rho)}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,x',y',z,y,x}e^{-ik\cdot(w-y')}e^{-ik\cdot(z-y')\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{1}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\notag\\
& {\color{red}+}\Big(-i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\bigg]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\notag\\
& {\color{red}+}\Big(-i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\bigg]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-III.III-2}
\end{align}
Genuine two-$\rho$ terms:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.III}_{2}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(2\rho)} & =\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,x',y',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot(w-y')}e^{-ik\cdot(z-y')\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\frac{1}{p^{+}\,(x-z)^{2}+k^{+}\,(x-w)^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\Bigg\{\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg[\frac{p^{+}\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{(z-w)^{2}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})^{2}}\notag\\
&+\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big((x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)-\frac{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\Big)\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\Big((x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)+\frac{1}{2}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\frac{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\Big)\bigg]\notag\\
&+\frac{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}{(p^{+}+k^{+})\,(x-w)^{2}}\Big[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)+\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\Big]\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{k^{+}}\Big[(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\notag\\
&+(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\Big]\Bigg\}\notag\\
&\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y')\}\notag\\ &+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big]_{+}\int_{w,x',y',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot(w-y')}e^{-ik\cdot(z-y')\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\frac{1}{(x-w)^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\Big[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)+\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\Big]\notag\\
&\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y')\}\notag\\ &+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+\,2}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,x',y',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot(w-y')}e^{-ik\cdot(z-y')\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\,\frac{p^{+}}{p^{+}\,(x-z)^{2}+k^{+}\,(x-w)^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\Big[(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\Big]\notag\\
&\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y')\}\notag\\ &+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\notag\\
&-\Big[(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\Big]\notag\\
&\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)\}\notag\\ &-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\bigg\}\notag\\
& +\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,x',w',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\,\frac{(x-w)^{2}}{\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\right]\notag \\ &  \bigg[\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)-\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)\bigg]   \times\notag \\ & \frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)\}\notag\\ &-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\,+{\rm c.c.}\,,\label{eq:E-III.III-2o}
\end{align}
\subsubsection*{Row IV}
\textsc{NA}
\subsubsection*{Row V}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ part:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.V}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(3\rho)} &=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-y')}e^{-ik\cdot(z-y')\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{1}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(-i\Big)\,{\color{red}\sigma}(x',x,y)\Big[-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(y')U(x)\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w)U(x)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w)U(x)\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(-i\Big)\,{\color{red}\sigma}(x',x,y)\Big[-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(x)\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w)U(x)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(w)U(x)\}\Big]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-III.V-3}

Two $\rho$ terms after symmetrization: 
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.V}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(2\rho)}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-y')}e^{-ik\cdot(z-y')\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\Bigg[\notag\\

&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(w)\}\Big]\notag\\

& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(w)\}\Big]\notag\\
& {\color{red}+}\Big(-\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(w)\}\Big]\bigg]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(w)\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\notag\\
& {\color{red}+}\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\notag\\
& {\color{red}+}\Big(-\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(w)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(z)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)U(w)\}\Big]\bigg]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-III.V-2}
\end{align}

\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.VI}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(3\rho)}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-y')}e^{-ik\cdot(z-y')\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\Bigg[\notag\\

& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(y)U(x')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(y)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(y)\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(-i\Big)\,{\color{red}\sigma}(x',y,x)\Big[-{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')U(x)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(x)\}\notag\\
& +{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(y)U(x')D^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)U(y)\}\Big]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-III.VI-3}

Two $\rho$ term after symmetrization: 
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.VI}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(2\rho)}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-y')}e^{-ik\cdot(z-y')\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{1}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&-\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(z)\}\Big]\notag\\
& {\color{red}+}\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}-N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y')\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(z)\}\Big]\notag\\
& {\color{red}+}\Big(-\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')\}\notag\\
& -{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}+{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(y')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')U(z)\}\Big]\bigg]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}\Big]\notag\\
& {\color{red}+}\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')\}-{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(x')T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')U(z)\}\Big]\notag\\
& {\color{red}+}\Big(-\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)\}+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\{U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}\notag\\
& -2{\rm Tr}\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)U(y)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)U(z)\}\Big]\bigg]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:E-III.VI-2}
\end{align}
\section{Final result with factorised Wilson-line structures}\label{sec:F}
\noindent This section repeats the result of Secs.~6 and 7 (after the exact cancellations and the symmetrisation of the charges), Group by Group and Row by Row, with (a) every colour structure written as a sum of products of \emph{differences} of Wilson lines, as in the LO structure $\big[U^{ce'}(y')-U^{ce'}(x')\big]\big[U^{ce}(y)-U^{ce}(x)\big]$, and (b) every longitudinal integral written with the momentum fraction $\xi$. Everything else (prefactors, transverse kernels, phases, the order of the charges, the c.c.) is that of Secs.~6 and 7. Every rewriting is an exact identity (any $N_{c}$, nothing dropped; checks in Sec.~\ref{sec:F3}). The conventions of Sec.~\ref{sec:conv2} apply, in particular: principal value in Group I Row III, pure glue, and the terms that must be integrated together.

\paragraph*{Longitudinal momentum fraction.} Two definitions (author, 2026-11-23):
\begin{itemize}
\item Group II Row IV (as for DGLAP, Sec.~3): $\xi=\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}+k^{+}}$, the fraction of the parent momentum carried by the observed gluon ($k^{+}/\xi$ is the parent momentum).
\item All other rows: $\xi=\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}$.
\end{itemize}
Jacobians and limits: Group I: $dp^{+}=k^{+}d\xi$, $0\le\xi\le1$; Group III: $dp^{+}=k^{+}d\xi$, $0\le\xi<\infty$; Group II Row IV: $p^{+}=k^{+}\frac{1-\xi}{\xi}$, $dp^{+}=-k^{+}\frac{d\xi}{\xi^{2}}$, $p^{+}=0\leftrightarrow\xi=1$, $p^{+}=\vee-k^{+}\leftrightarrow\xi=\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}$, so that
\begin{align}
\int_{0}^{\vee-k^{+}}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\,\frac{p^{+}}{(p^{+}+k^{+})^{2}}\,e^{-ik\cdot(y-y')}\,e^{-ik\cdot(y-y')\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}=\int_{k^{+}/\vee}^{1}\frac{d\xi}{2\pi}\,\frac{1-\xi}{\xi}\,e^{-ik\cdot(y-y')\frac{1}{\xi}}\,.\notag
\end{align}
The recoil phases become $e^{-ik\cdot(a)\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}}=e^{-ik\cdot(a)\,\xi}$. The plus prescriptions become (same subtraction points; the prescription acts on the whole $\xi$-dependent function $f$ of the term):
\begin{align}
\int_{0}^{k^{+}}dp^{+}\Big[\frac{1}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}f&=\int_{0}^{1}d\xi\Big[\frac{1}{\xi}\Big]_{+}f\,,\qquad \int_{0}^{k^{+}}dp^{+}\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}}\Big]_{+}f=k^{+}\int_{0}^{1}d\xi\Big[\frac{1}{\xi}\Big]_{+}f\,,\qquad \int_{0}^{1}d\xi\Big[\frac{1}{\xi}\Big]_{+}f(\xi)=\int_{0}^{1}\frac{d\xi}{\xi}\big[f(\xi)-f(0)\big]\,,\notag\\
\int_{0}^{k^{+}}dp^{+}\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{k^{+}-p^{+}}\Big]_{+}f&=k^{+}\int_{0}^{1}d\xi\Big[\frac{1}{1-\xi}\Big]_{+}f\,,\qquad \int_{0}^{1}d\xi\Big[\frac{1}{1-\xi}\Big]_{+}f(\xi)=\int_{0}^{1}\frac{d\xi}{1-\xi}\big[f(\xi)-f(1)\big]\,,\notag\\
\int_{0}^{\infty}dp^{+}\Big[\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Big]_{+}f&=\int_{0}^{\infty}d\xi\Big[\frac{1}{\xi(1+\xi)}\Big]_{+}f\,,\qquad \int_{0}^{\infty}d\xi\Big[\frac{1}{\xi(1+\xi)}\Big]_{+}f(\xi)=\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{d\xi}{\xi(1+\xi)}\big[f(\xi)-f(0)\big]\,,\notag
\end{align}
and the instantaneous terms of Group III: $\int_{0}^{\infty}d\xi\,e^{-ik\cdot(z-y')\,\xi}=\pi\delta(s)-i\,\mathcal{P}\frac{1}{s}$, $s=k\cdot(z-y')$ (with the conditions of Sec.~\ref{sec:conv2}). The energy denominators become $\frac{1}{p^{+}+k^{+}}=\frac{1}{k^{+}(1+\xi)}$, $\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}(y-x)^{2}+(k^{+}-p^{+})(y-z)^{2}}=\frac{1}{\xi(y-x)^{2}+(1-\xi)(y-z)^{2}}$, $\frac{1}{p^{+}(x-z)^{2}+k^{+}(x-w)^{2}}=\frac{1}{k^{+}}\frac{1}{\xi(x-z)^{2}+(x-w)^{2}}$; the factors $k^{+}$ are collected in the prefactors (every row has the prefactor $\frac{1}{k^{+}}$).

\paragraph*{Colour structures.} The identities used (exact, any $N_{c}$): $f^{gde}U^{db}(p)U^{ec}(p)=U^{gh}(p)f^{hbc}$ (invariance of $f$), $U^{mh}(p)U^{kh}(p)=\delta^{mk}$, $f^{acd}f^{bcd}=N_{c}\delta^{ab}$; e.g.\ $f^{hbc}U^{gh}(w)-f^{gde}U^{db}(x)U^{ec}(y)=-f^{gde}\big\{[U^{db}(x)-U^{db}(w)]U^{ec}(y)+U^{db}(w)[U^{ec}(y)-U^{ec}(w)]\big\}$. In the singlet form the contractions $C^{abc}d^{abc}$ (three-$\rho$ part) and $C^{abc}f^{abc}$ at coinciding charges (contact part) of a product of three matrices $A,B,C$ (each a product of Wilson lines and differences, one of them, $O$, a Wilson line or a product of Wilson lines) are written as
\begin{align}
f^{ijk}d^{lmn}O^{il}B^{jm}C^{kn}=i\,{\rm Tr}\big\{T^{h}O^{\dagger}C\,D^{h}B^{\dagger}O\big\}\,,\qquad f^{ijk}f^{lmn}O^{il}B^{jm}C^{kn}={\rm Tr}\big\{T^{e}O^{\dagger}C\,T^{e}B^{\dagger}O\big\}\,,\notag
\end{align}
with $B$, $C$ the differences, e.g.\ ${\rm Tr}\big\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)\big(U(z)-U(y)\big)D^{h}\big(U^{\dagger}(x')-U^{\dagger}(w')\big)U(x)\big\}$. Each row's singlet equation has the form of Sec.~7 with $\frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}(t\,i)\,\sigma\,[\dots]$, $t=2$ for the rows with an anticommutator and $t=1$ for the ordered products, and the contact coefficients $(\pm i)$, $(\pm\frac{i}{2})$ of Sec.~7.

\fixnote{\textbf{What cannot be written as a product of differences only.} (1) Every three-$\rho$ colour tensor $C^{abc}$ has three adjoint indices, one for each charge. In every term exactly two of the three colour legs carry a difference of Wilson lines; the third leg carries one Wilson line $U(p)$ (or a product $U^{\dagger}(z)U(\dots)$, or the unit matrix). This third Wilson line cannot be turned into a difference: writing $U(p)=[U(p)-U(q)]+U(q)$ adds a term that does not vanish. Therefore the three-$\rho$ structures are given as ``$[U-U]\times[U-U]\times U\times f$'' and, in singlet form, as traces with two differences and one Wilson line ($O$ above). (2) In Group II Row I, Group III Rows II and III (second part) and in the contact terms of Group II Row I and Group III Row II the factorisation produces products $U^{\dagger}(z)\,[U(z)-U(y)]$ and $[U^{\dagger}(w)-U^{\dagger}(z)]\,U(x')$, i.e.\ a difference times a Wilson line in one leg; this is the closest exact form (the same reason). (3) In the three-$\rho$ part of Group I Row II the two traces of the factorised form add up to a shorter expression with one difference (the term $f^{hbc}U^{gh}(w)$ of the tensor drops out of $C^{abc}d^{abc}$ because $f^{hbc}d^{abc}=0$, Sec.~7); the factorised form is kept for uniformity, both are exact. (4) The genuine two-$\rho$ structures and all contact (two-$\rho$) structures are sums of products of two differences, with $f$, $N_{c}$ and one Wilson line; nothing is left unfactorised.}

\subsection{$\rho$-explicit form (after cancellation and symmetrisation)}\label{sec:F1}
\noindent Prefactors, transverse kernels and phases as in Sec.~6; the colour tensors are written with differences of Wilson lines and the longitudinal integrals with $\xi$. Complex conjugate implied in Groups I and III (written as $+{\rm c.c.}$).
\subsubsection{Group I}
\paragraph*{Row I}
\textsc{NA}
\paragraph*{Row II}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.II}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{3\rho}&=-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{64\pi^{6}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\int_{x,y,x'}\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\int d^{2}p\,d^{2}q\;e^{iq\cdot(w-y)}\,e^{ip\cdot(y-x)}\,\frac{(x'-w')^{i}}{(x'-w')^{2}\,\mathbf{q}^{2}}\,\frac{1}{2}\bigg[\frac{R^{i}(\mathbf{q},\mathbf{p})}{\mathbf{p}^{2}}-\frac{R^{i}(\mathbf{q},\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})}{(\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})^{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times 2\,\Bigg\{-\Big[U^{ga}(x')-U^{ga}(w')\Big]f^{gde}\bigg[\Big[U^{db}(x)-U^{db}(w)\Big]U^{ec}(y)+U^{db}(w)\Big[U^{ec}(y)-U^{ec}(w)\Big]\bigg]\Bigg\}\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:H-I.II-3}
\end{align}
Two-$\rho$ terms from symmetrisation:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.II}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{2\rho}&=-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{64\pi^{6}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\int_{x,y,x'}\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\int d^{2}p\,d^{2}q\;e^{iq\cdot(w-y)}\,e^{ip\cdot(y-x)}\,\frac{(x'-w')^{i}}{(x'-w')^{2}\,\mathbf{q}^{2}}\,\frac{1}{2}\bigg[\frac{R^{i}(\mathbf{q},\mathbf{p})}{\mathbf{p}^{2}}-\frac{R^{i}(\mathbf{q},\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})}{(\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})^{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times i\,\Bigg\{-\Big[U^{ga}(x')-U^{ga}(w')\Big]f^{gde}\bigg[\Big[U^{db}(x)-U^{db}(w)\Big]U^{ec}(y)+U^{db}(w)\Big[U^{ec}(y)-U^{ec}(w)\Big]\bigg]\Bigg\}\times\notag \\ & \Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}+f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:H-I.II-2}
\end{align}
Genuine two-$\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.II}_{1}}{d^{3}k} & = +\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}\,N_{c}}{32\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\Big(-\frac{67}{9}+\frac{\pi^{2}}{3}\Big)\int_{w,w',x,x'} e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\;\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\notag \\
& \times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\Big[U^{ab}(w)-U^{ab}(x)\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{b}(x) + {\rm c.c.}\label{eq:H-I.II-2o}
\end{align}
\paragraph*{Row III}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.III}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{3\rho}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\int_{x,y,z,x'}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{{\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)}\notag\\
&{-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}}\notag\\
&{+\frac{\arccos\left(\frac{(x-w)^{2}-(x-w)\cdot (x-z)}{\sqrt{(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w)^{2}}}\right)}{\sqrt{(x-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-\left[(x-w)\cdot (x-z)\right]^{2}}\,(z-w)^{2}}\,\bigg[\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(x-w)}\notag\\
&{-\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-z)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(x-z)}\notag\\
&{+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)-\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)\bigg]}\notag\\
&{-\frac{1}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)}\notag\\
&{+\bigg[-\frac{1}{2}\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}-\int_{0}^{1}d\xi\Big[\frac{1}{\xi}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot(z-w)\,\xi}\bigg]\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)}\notag\\
&{+\int_{0}^{1}d\xi\Big[\frac{1}{\xi}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot(z-w)\,\xi}\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \Bigg\{\Big[U^{ga}(x')-U^{ga}(w')\Big]f^{ghd}\,U^{db}(x)\Big[U^{hc}(z)-U^{hc}(y)\Big]\Bigg\}\,\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&+\bigg\{{-\int_{0}^{1}d\xi\Big[\frac{1}{\xi}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot(z-w)\,\xi}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \Bigg\{\Big[U^{ga}(x')-U^{ga}(w')\Big]f^{ghd}\,U^{db}(x)\Big[U^{hc}(w)-U^{hc}(y)\Big]\Bigg\}\,\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:H-I.III-3}
\end{align}
Two-$\rho$ terms from symmetrisation:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.III}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{2\rho} &=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\int_{x,y,z,x'}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{{\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)}\notag\\
&{-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}}\notag\\
&{+\frac{\arccos\left(\frac{(x-w)^{2}-(x-w)\cdot (x-z)}{\sqrt{(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w)^{2}}}\right)}{\sqrt{(x-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-\left[(x-w)\cdot (x-z)\right]^{2}}\,(z-w)^{2}}\,\bigg[\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(x-w)}\notag\\
&{-\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-z)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(x-z)}\notag\\
&{+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)-\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)\bigg]}\notag\\
&{-\frac{1}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)}\notag\\
&{+\bigg[-\frac{1}{2}\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}-\int_{0}^{1}d\xi\Big[\frac{1}{\xi}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot(z-w)\,\xi}\bigg]\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)}\notag\\
&{+\int_{0}^{1}d\xi\Big[\frac{1}{\xi}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot(z-w)\,\xi}\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{i}{2}\,\Bigg\{\Big[U^{ga}(x')-U^{ga}(w')\Big]f^{ghd}\,U^{db}(x)\Big[U^{hc}(z)-U^{hc}(y)\Big]\Bigg\}\notag\\&\quad\times\Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
& +f^{bcr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y)\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{r}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym} +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{{-\int_{0}^{1}d\xi\Big[\frac{1}{\xi}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot(z-w)\,\xi}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{i}{2}\,\Bigg\{\Big[U^{ga}(x')-U^{ga}(w')\Big]f^{ghd}\,U^{db}(x)\Big[U^{hc}(w)-U^{hc}(y)\Big]\Bigg\}\notag\\&\quad\times\Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
& +f^{bcr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y)\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{r}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym} +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:H-I.III-2}
\end{align}
\paragraph*{Row IV}
\textsc{NA} - canceled with Group III, Row I
\paragraph*{Row V}
\begin{align}
C^{abc}_{{\rm I.V}}&\equiv \Big[U^{ga}(x')-U^{ga}(w')\Big]f^{gde}\Big[U^{dc}(x)\,U^{eb}(z)-U^{eb}(y)\,U^{dc}(y')\Big]\notag
\end{align}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.V}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{3\rho}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{1}\frac{d\xi}{2\pi}\int_{x,z,y,y',x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot(z-w')}\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{e^{-ik\cdot(x-z)\,\xi}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\notag\\
&{-\Big[\frac{1}{\xi}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot(x-z)\,\xi}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)}\notag\\
&{-\Big[\frac{1}{\xi}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot(x-z)}e^{-ik\cdot(z-x)\,\xi}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times 2\,\Bigg\{\Big[U^{ga}(x')-U^{ga}(w')\Big]f^{gde}\bigg[\Big[U^{dc}(x)-U^{dc}(y')\Big]U^{eb}(z)+U^{dc}(y')\Big[U^{eb}(z)-U^{eb}(y)\Big]\bigg]\Bigg\}\times\,\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\rho^{c}(y')\big\}_{\rm sym}+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:H-I.V-3}
\end{align}
Two-$\rho$ terms from symmetrisation:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.V}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{2\rho}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{1}\frac{d\xi}{2\pi}\int_{x,z,y,y',x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot(z-w')}\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{e^{-ik\cdot(x-z)\,\xi}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\notag\\
&{-\Big[\frac{1}{\xi}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot(x-z)\,\xi}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)}\notag\\
&{-\Big[\frac{1}{\xi}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot(x-z)}e^{-ik\cdot(z-x)\,\xi}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times i\,\Bigg\{\Big[U^{ga}(x')-U^{ga}(w')\Big]f^{gde}\bigg[\Big[U^{dc}(x)-U^{dc}(y')\Big]U^{eb}(z)+U^{dc}(y')\Big[U^{eb}(z)-U^{eb}(y)\Big]\bigg]\Bigg\}\notag\\&\quad\times\Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(y')\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
& +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y')\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:H-I.V-2}
\end{align}
and the genuine two-$\rho$:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.V}_{2}}{d^{3}k} & =\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{1}\frac{d\xi}{2\pi}\int_{x,y,z,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot(z-w')}\notag\\
