&\times\Bigg\{\frac{e^{-ik\cdot(x-z)\,\xi}}{\xi\,(y-x)^{2}+(1-\xi)\,(y-z)^{2}}\bigg[\xi(1-\xi)\,\frac{(y-x)^{2}-(y-z)^{2}}{(x-z)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\notag\\
&+(1-\xi)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\Big[(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)+\frac{1}{2}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)-\frac{(y-x)^{2}-(y-z)^{2}}{2\,(x-z)^{2}}\Big]\notag\\
&+\xi\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\Big[(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\frac{1}{2}(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)-\frac{(y-x)^{2}-(y-z)^{2}}{2\,(x-z)^{2}}\Big]\notag\\
&+\frac{(y-x)^{2}}{(y-z)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\,(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)\Big]\notag\\
&+\frac{(y-z)^{2}}{(y-x)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}-\frac{1}{2}\,(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\Big]\notag\\
&-(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')+\frac{1}{2}\,(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\notag\\
&-(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')-\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\bigg]\notag\\
&{-\Big[\frac{1}{\xi}\Big]_{+}\frac{e^{-ik\cdot(x-z)\,\xi}}{(y-z)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\,(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)\Big]}\notag\\
&{-\Big[\frac{1}{1-\xi}\Big]_{+}\frac{e^{-ik\cdot(x-z)\,\xi}}{(y-x)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}-\frac{1}{2}\,(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\Big]}\Bigg\}\notag\\
&\times\Bigg\{-\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]f^{adc}f^{fbe}\,U^{db}(x)\Big[U^{ce}(z)-U^{ce}(x)\Big]+N_{c}\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\Big[U^{af}(y)-U^{af}(x)\Big]\Bigg\}\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{f}(y)\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}N_{c}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot(z-w')}\,e^{-ik\cdot(x-z)}\,\frac{11}{6}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)\,\frac{(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{(x-z)^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\Big[U^{af}(y)-U^{af}(x)\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{f}(y)\,+ {\rm c.c.}
\label{eq:H-I.V-2o}
\end{align}
\subsubsection{Group II}
\paragraph*{Row I}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{3\rho}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,w,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{-\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\&\quad\times 2\,\Bigg\{\Big[U^{ga}(w')-U^{ga}(x')\Big]\bigg[\Big[U^{gk}(w)-U^{gk}(z)\Big]f^{kbc}-f^{gkl}\,U^{kb}(z)\Big[U^{lc}(x)-U^{lc}(z)\Big]\bigg]\notag\\&\qquad+U^{ga}(x')\bigg[U^{mh}(z)\Big[U^{mb}(z)-U^{mb}(y)\Big]\Big[U^{gk}(w)-U^{gk}(z)\Big]f^{khc}\notag\\&\qquad\quad-f^{gml}\Big[U^{mb}(z)-U^{mb}(y)\Big]\Big[U^{lc}(x)-U^{lc}(z)\Big]\bigg]\Bigg\}\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w')^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w')\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w')^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\&\quad\times 2\,\Bigg\{\Big[U^{gb}(w)-U^{gb}(x')\Big]\bigg[\Big[U^{gk}(w')-U^{gk}(z)\Big]f^{kca}-f^{gkl}\,U^{kc}(z)\Big[U^{la}(x)-U^{la}(z)\Big]\bigg]\notag\\&\qquad+U^{gb}(x')\bigg[U^{mh}(z)\Big[U^{mc}(z)-U^{mc}(y)\Big]\Big[U^{gk}(w')-U^{gk}(z)\Big]f^{kha}\notag\\&\qquad\quad-f^{gml}\Big[U^{mc}(z)-U^{mc}(y)\Big]\Big[U^{la}(x)-U^{la}(z)\Big]\bigg]\Bigg\}\big\{\rho^{a}(x)\rho^{b}(x')\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Bigg]\label{eq:H-II.I-3}
\end{align}
Two-$\rho$ terms from symmetrization: 
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{2\rho}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,w,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{-\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times i\,\Bigg\{\Big[U^{ga}(w')-U^{ga}(x')\Big]\bigg[\Big[U^{gk}(w)-U^{gk}(z)\Big]f^{kbc}-f^{gkl}\,U^{kb}(z)\Big[U^{lc}(x)-U^{lc}(z)\Big]\bigg]\notag\\&\qquad+U^{ga}(x')\bigg[U^{mh}(z)\Big[U^{mb}(z)-U^{mb}(y)\Big]\Big[U^{gk}(w)-U^{gk}(z)\Big]f^{khc}\notag\\&\qquad\quad-f^{gml}\Big[U^{mb}(z)-U^{mb}(y)\Big]\Big[U^{lc}(x)-U^{lc}(z)\Big]\bigg]\Bigg\}\notag \\ & \Big[f^{bcr}\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{r}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym} +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w')^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w')\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w')^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times i\,\Bigg\{\Big[U^{gb}(w)-U^{gb}(x')\Big]\bigg[\Big[U^{gk}(w')-U^{gk}(z)\Big]f^{kca}-f^{gkl}\,U^{kc}(z)\Big[U^{la}(x)-U^{la}(z)\Big]\bigg]\notag\\&\qquad+U^{gb}(x')\bigg[U^{mh}(z)\Big[U^{mc}(z)-U^{mc}(y)\Big]\Big[U^{gk}(w')-U^{gk}(z)\Big]f^{kha}\notag\\&\qquad\quad-f^{gml}\Big[U^{mc}(z)-U^{mc}(y)\Big]\Big[U^{la}(x)-U^{la}(z)\Big]\bigg]\Bigg\}\notag \\ & \Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x-x')\big\{\rho^{r}(x)\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym} +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x)\rho^{b}(x')\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\Bigg]\label{eq:H-II.I-2}
\end{align}
Genuine $2-\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2a}}{d^{3}k}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-w^{\prime})}}\,\frac{1}{\big[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\right]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\frac{1}{2}\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\notag \\ & +\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime}) -\frac{1}{4}\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\notag \\ & +(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})+\frac{1}{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{2}\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)-\frac{1}{4}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\Bigg]\notag \\ & \times\bigg[\Big[U^{ac'}(w')-U^{ac'}(z)\Big]f^{c'bd'}-f^{ac'e'}\,U^{c'b}(z)\Big[U^{e'd'}(x')-U^{e'd'}(z)\Big]\bigg]\notag\\&\qquad\times\bigg[\Big[U^{ac}(w)-U^{ac}(z)\Big]f^{cbd}-f^{ace}\,U^{cb}(z)\Big[U^{ed}(x)-U^{ed}(z)\Big]\bigg]\rho^{d'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(x)\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2b}}{d^{3}k}&= -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-w^{\prime})}}\,\frac{1}{\big[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-w)^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\right]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\frac{1}{2}\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\notag \\ & +\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime}) -\frac{1}{4}\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\notag \\ & +(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})+\frac{1}{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{2}\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)-\frac{1}{4}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\Bigg]\notag\\
&\times\bigg[\Big[U^{ac'}(w')-U^{ac'}(z)\Big]f^{c'bd'}-f^{ac'e'}\,U^{c'b}(z)\Big[U^{e'd'}(x')-U^{e'd'}(z)\Big]\bigg]\notag\\&\qquad\times\bigg[\Big[U^{ac}(w)-U^{ac}(z)\Big]f^{cbd}-f^{ace}\,U^{cb}(z)\Big[U^{ed}(x)-U^{ed}(z)\Big]\bigg]\rho^{d'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(x)\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2c}}{d^{3}k} &= \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-w^{\prime})}}\,\frac{1}{\big[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\right]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[-\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg[\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\,(z-w)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(z-w)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,(x-z)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})}{2\,(x-z)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)}{4\,(x-z)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\bigg]\notag\\
&-\frac{(x-w)^{2}}{(x-z)^{2}}\bigg[\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x-z)\,(z-w)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}-\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x-z)\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(z-w)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & -\frac{(x-w)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})\,(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)}{2\,(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)}{4\,(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\bigg]\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\{(x-z)^{2}\bigg[\frac{(x-w)\cdot(z-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{4\,(x-z)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag\\ &+\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(z-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\bigg]-(x-w)^{2}\bigg[\frac{(x-z)\cdot(z-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & -\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{4\,(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & -\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}-\frac{1}{2}\,\frac{(x-z)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}\Bigg]\notag\\
&\times\bigg[\Big[U^{ac'}(w')-U^{ac'}(z)\Big]f^{c'bd'}-f^{ac'e'}\,U^{c'b}(z)\Big[U^{e'd'}(x')-U^{e'd'}(z)\Big]\bigg]\notag\\&\qquad\times\bigg[\Big[U^{ac}(w)-U^{ac}(z)\Big]f^{cbd}-f^{ace}\,U^{cb}(z)\Big[U^{ed}(x)-U^{ed}(z)\Big]\bigg]\rho^{d'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(x)\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2d}}{d^{3}k}&= \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-w^{\prime})}}\,\frac{1}{\big[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\right]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\bigg[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})+\frac{1}{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\notag \\ & +\frac{1}{2}\,(x-w)\cdot(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})+\frac{1}{4}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\bigg]\notag\\
&+\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\bigg[(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})-\frac{1}{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)+\frac{1}{4}\,(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\bigg]\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}-(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\bigg\{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\bigg[\frac{(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag\\ &+\frac{(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w)^{2}}\bigg]\notag\\
&-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\bigg[\frac{(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}-\frac{(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}-\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w)^{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\,\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}\Bigg]\notag\\
&\times\bigg[\Big[U^{ac'}(w')-U^{ac'}(z)\Big]f^{c'bd'}-f^{ac'e'}\,U^{c'b}(z)\Big[U^{e'd'}(x')-U^{e'd'}(z)\Big]\bigg]\notag\\&\qquad\times\bigg[\Big[U^{ac}(w)-U^{ac}(z)\Big]f^{cbd}-f^{ace}\,U^{cb}(z)\Big[U^{ed}(x)-U^{ed}(z)\Big]\bigg]\rho^{d'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(x)\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2e}}{d^{3}k}&= -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot{(w-w^{\prime})}}\,\frac{1}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\bigg[\Big[U^{ac'}(w')-U^{ac'}(z)\Big]f^{c'bd'}-f^{ac'e'}\,U^{c'b}(z)\Big[U^{e'd'}(x')-U^{e'd'}(z)\Big]\bigg]\notag\\&\qquad\times\bigg[\Big[U^{ac}(w)-U^{ac}(z)\Big]f^{cbd}-f^{ace}\,U^{cb}(z)\Big[U^{ed}(x)-U^{ed}(z)\Big]\bigg]\rho^{d'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(x)\notag
\end{align}
\paragraph*{Row II}
\textsc{NA}
\paragraph*{Row III}
\textsc{NA}
\paragraph*{Row IV}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.IV}}{d^{3}k}= &  +\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}\,N_{c}}{4\pi^{3}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{k^{+}/\vee}^{1}\frac{d\xi}{2\pi}\,\frac{1-\xi}{\xi}\int_{x,y,x',y'}e^{-ik\cdot(y-y')\frac{1}{\xi}}\Big[\mathcal{K}(x-y)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\notag \\ & -2\,\mathcal{K}(x-y)\cdot(y'-y)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-y)\Big]\times\Big[U^{ce'}(y') - U^{ce'}(x')\Big]\Big[U^{ce}(y) - U^{ce}(x)\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\rho^{e}(x)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}\,N_{c}}{2\pi^{3}}\,\frac{1}{k^{2}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,x',z}\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\Big[U^{ce'}(z)-U^{ce'}(x')\Big]\Big[U^{ce}(z)-U^{ce}(x)\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\rho^{e}(x)
\label{eq:H-II.IVrem}
\end{align}
\subsubsection{Group III}
\paragraph*{Row I}
\textsc{NA} -- Cancels with Group I, Row IV
\paragraph*{Row II}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.II}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{3\rho}&=-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,x',z,w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\,\bigg\{\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\&\quad\times \Bigg\{-U^{gb}(x')\,U^{mh}(z)\Big[U^{ma}(z)-U^{ma}(y)\Big]\Big[U^{gk}(w)-U^{gk}(z)\Big]f^{khc}\notag\\&\qquad+U^{gb}(x')\,f^{gml}\Big[U^{ma}(z)-U^{ma}(y)\Big]\Big[U^{lc}(x)-U^{lc}(z)\Big]\Bigg\}\,\big\{\rho^{a}(y)\rho^{b}(x')\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:H-III.II-3}
\end{align}
Two-$\rho$ terms after symmetrization:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.II}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{2\rho}&=-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,x',z,w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\,\bigg\{\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\}\times\,\notag\\
& \frac{i}{2}\,\Bigg\{-U^{gb}(x')\,U^{mh}(z)\Big[U^{ma}(z)-U^{ma}(y)\Big]\Big[U^{gk}(w)-U^{gk}(z)\Big]f^{khc}\notag\\&\qquad+U^{gb}(x')\,f^{gml}\Big[U^{ma}(z)-U^{ma}(y)\Big]\Big[U^{lc}(x)-U^{lc}(z)\Big]\Bigg\}\notag \\ & \Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x')\big\{\rho^{r}(y)\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym} +f^{bcr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{a}(y)\rho^{r}(x')\big\}_{\rm sym} +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(y)\rho^{b}(x')\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:H-III.II-2}
\end{align}
\paragraph*{Row III}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.III}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{3\rho}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{d\xi}{2\pi}\int_{w,x',y',z,y,x}e^{-ik\cdot(w-y')}e^{-ik\cdot(z-y')\,\xi}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{1}{1+\xi}\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&{-\Big[\frac{1}{\xi\,(1+\xi)}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times 2\,\Bigg\{f^{mnk}\Big[U^{ma}(y')-U^{ma}(x')\Big]\bigg[\Big[U^{nc}(z)-U^{nc}(x)\Big]U^{kb}(w)+U^{nc}(x)\Big[U^{kb}(w)-U^{kb}(y)\Big]\bigg]\notag\\&\qquad\Bigg\}\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times 2\,\Bigg\{f^{mnk}\Big[U^{ma}(z)-U^{ma}(x')\Big]\bigg[\Big[U^{nc}(z)-U^{nc}(x)\Big]U^{kb}(w)+U^{nc}(x)\Big[U^{kb}(w)-U^{kb}(y)\Big]\bigg]\notag\\&\qquad\Bigg\}\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:H-III.III-3}
\end{align}
 Two-$\rho$ terms after symmetrisation:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.III}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{2\rho}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{d\xi}{2\pi}\int_{w,x',y',z,y,x}e^{-ik\cdot(w-y')}e^{-ik\cdot(z-y')\,\xi}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{1}{1+\xi}\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&{-\Big[\frac{1}{\xi\,(1+\xi)}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times i\,\Bigg\{f^{mnk}\Big[U^{ma}(y')-U^{ma}(x')\Big]\bigg[\Big[U^{nc}(z)-U^{nc}(x)\Big]U^{kb}(w)+U^{nc}(x)\Big[U^{kb}(w)-U^{kb}(y)\Big]\bigg]\notag\\&\qquad\Bigg\}\notag \\ & \Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym} +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\&\quad\times i\,\Bigg\{f^{mnk}\Big[U^{ma}(z)-U^{ma}(x')\Big]\bigg[\Big[U^{nc}(z)-U^{nc}(x)\Big]U^{kb}(w)+U^{nc}(x)\Big[U^{kb}(w)-U^{kb}(y)\Big]\bigg]\notag\\&\qquad\Bigg\}\notag \\ & \Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym} +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:H-III.III-2}
\end{align}
Genuine $2-\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.III}_{2}}{d^{3}k} & =\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{d\xi}{2\pi}\int_{w,x',y',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot(w-y')}e^{-ik\cdot(z-y')\,\xi}\,\frac{1}{\xi\,(x-z)^{2}+(x-w)^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\Bigg\{\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg[\frac{\xi\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{(z-w)^{2}\,(1+\xi)^{2}}\notag\\
&+\frac{\xi}{1+\xi}\Big((x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)-\frac{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\Big)\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{1+\xi}\Big((x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)+\frac{1}{2}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\frac{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\Big)\bigg]\notag\\
&+\frac{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}{(1+\xi)\,(x-w)^{2}}\Big[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)+\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\Big]\notag\\
&-\Big[(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\notag\\
&+(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\Big]\Bigg\}\notag\\
&\times\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{d'b}(z)\Big[U^{c'e'}(y')-U^{c'e'}(x')\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\notag\\
&\times\bigg[\Big[U^{ac}(w)-U^{ac}(z)\Big]f^{cbd}-f^{ace}\,U^{cb}(z)\Big[U^{ed}(x)-U^{ed}(z)\Big]\bigg]\rho^{d}(x)\notag\\
&{-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{d\xi}{2\pi}\Big[\frac{1}{\xi\,(1+\xi)}\Big]_{+}\int_{w,x',y',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot(w-y')}e^{-ik\cdot(z-y')\,\xi}\,\frac{1}{(x-w)^{2}}}\notag\\
&{\times\Big[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)+\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\Big]}\notag\\
&\times\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{d'b}(z)\Big[U^{c'e'}(y')-U^{c'e'}(x')\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\notag\\
&\times\bigg[\Big[U^{ac}(w)-U^{ac}(z)\Big]f^{cbd}-f^{ace}\,U^{cb}(z)\Big[U^{ed}(x)-U^{ed}(z)\Big]\bigg]\rho^{d}(x)\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{d\xi}{2\pi}\int_{w,x',y',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot(w-y')}e^{-ik\cdot(z-y')\,\xi}\,\frac{\xi}{\xi\,(x-z)^{2}+(x-w)^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\Big[(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\Big]\notag\\
&\times\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{d'b}(z)\Big[U^{c'e'}(y')-U^{c'e'}(x')\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\notag\\
&-\Big[(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\Big]\notag\\
&\times\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{d'b}(z)\Big[U^{c'e'}(z)-U^{c'e'}(x')\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times\bigg[\Big[U^{ac}(w)-U^{ac}(z)\Big]f^{cbd}-f^{ace}\,U^{cb}(z)\Big[U^{ed}(x)-U^{ed}(z)\Big]\bigg]\rho^{d}(x)\notag\\
& +\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,x',w',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\,\frac{(x-w)^{2}}{\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\right]\notag \\ &  \bigg[\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)-\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)\bigg]   \times\notag \\ & \,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{d'b}(z)\Big[U^{c'e'}(z)-U^{c'e'}(x')\Big]\rho^{e'}(x')\notag\\ &\times\bigg[\Big[U^{ac}(w)-U^{ac}(z)\Big]f^{cbd}-f^{ace}\,U^{cb}(z)\Big[U^{ed}(x)-U^{ed}(z)\Big]\bigg]\rho^{d}(x)\,+{\rm c.c.}
\label{eq:H-III.III-2o}
\end{align}
\paragraph*{Row IV}
\textsc{NA}
\paragraph*{Row V}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.V}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{3\rho}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{d\xi}{2\pi}\int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-y')}e^{-ik\cdot(z-y')\,\xi}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{1}{1+\xi}\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&{-\Big[\frac{1}{\xi\,(1+\xi)}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \Bigg\{f^{mkl}\Big[U^{ma}(y')-U^{ma}(x')\Big]U^{kb}(x)\Big[U^{lc}(y)-U^{lc}(w)\Big]\Bigg\}\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \Bigg\{f^{mkl}\Big[U^{ma}(z)-U^{ma}(x')\Big]U^{kb}(x)\Big[U^{lc}(y)-U^{lc}(w)\Big]\Bigg\}\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:H-III.V-3}
\end{align}
Two-$\rho$ terms from symmetrization: 
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.V}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{2\rho}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{d\xi}{2\pi}\int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-y')}e^{-ik\cdot(z-y')\,\xi}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{1}{1+\xi}\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&{-\Big[\frac{1}{\xi\,(1+\xi)}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{i}{2}\,\Bigg\{f^{mkl}\Big[U^{ma}(y')-U^{ma}(x')\Big]U^{kb}(x)\Big[U^{lc}(y)-U^{lc}(w)\Big]\Bigg\}\Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
& +f^{bcr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y)\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{r}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}+f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\&\quad\times \frac{i}{2}\,\Bigg\{f^{mkl}\Big[U^{ma}(z)-U^{ma}(x')\Big]U^{kb}(x)\Big[U^{lc}(y)-U^{lc}(w)\Big]\Bigg\}\times\,\notag \\ & \Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym} +f^{bcr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y)\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{r}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}+f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:H-III.V-2}
\end{align}
\paragraph*{Row VI}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.VI}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{3\rho}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{d\xi}{2\pi}\int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-y')}e^{-ik\cdot(z-y')\,\xi}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{1}{1+\xi}\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&{-\Big[\frac{1}{\xi\,(1+\xi)}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \Bigg\{f^{mnk}\Big[U^{ma}(y')-U^{ma}(x')\Big]U^{kb}(y)\Big[U^{nc}(x)-U^{nc}(z)\Big]\Bigg\}\,\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \Bigg\{f^{mnk}\Big[U^{ma}(z)-U^{ma}(x')\Big]U^{kb}(y)\Big[U^{nc}(x)-U^{nc}(z)\Big]\Bigg\}\,\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:H-III.VI-3}
\end{align}
Two-$\rho$ terms after symmetrisation:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.VI}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{2\rho}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{d\xi}{2\pi}\int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-y')}e^{-ik\cdot(z-y')\,\xi}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{1}{1+\xi}\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&{-\Big[\frac{1}{\xi\,(1+\xi)}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)}\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{i}{2}\,\Bigg\{f^{mnk}\Big[U^{ma}(y')-U^{ma}(x')\Big]U^{kb}(y)\Big[U^{nc}(x)-U^{nc}(z)\Big]\Bigg\}\notag\\&\quad\times\Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
& +f^{bcr}\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{r}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym} +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{i}{2}\,\Bigg\{f^{mnk}\Big[U^{ma}(z)-U^{ma}(x')\Big]U^{kb}(y)\Big[U^{nc}(x)-U^{nc}(z)\Big]\Bigg\}\notag\\&\quad\times\Big[f^{abr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{c}(x)\big\}_{\rm sym}\notag\\
& +f^{bcr}\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\big\{\rho^{a}(x')\rho^{r}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym} +f^{acr}\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\big\{\rho^{r}(x')\rho^{b}(y)\big\}_{\rm sym}\Big]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:H-III.VI-2}
\end{align}

\subsection{Average over the projectile: colour-singlet form}\label{sec:F2}
\noindent The same rows averaged over the projectile (Sec.~7 form; $n(x,y)$, $\sigma(x,y,z)$ as defined in Sec.~7), the traces written with differences of Wilson lines, the longitudinal integrals with $\xi$.
\subsubsection{Group I}
\paragraph*{Row I}
\textsc{NA}
\paragraph*{Row II}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ part:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.II}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(3\rho)}&=-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{64\pi^{6}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\int_{x,y,x'}\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\int d^{2}p\,d^{2}q\;e^{iq\cdot(w-y)}\,e^{ip\cdot(y-x)}\,\frac{(x'-w')^{i}}{(x'-w')^{2}\,\mathbf{q}^{2}}\,\frac{1}{2}\bigg[\frac{R^{i}(\mathbf{q},\mathbf{p})}{\mathbf{p}^{2}}-\frac{R^{i}(\mathbf{q},\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})}{(\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})^{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(+2i\Big)\,\sigma(x',x,y)\Big[-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(y)\big(U(x)-U(w)\big)D^{h}\big(U^{\dagger}(x')-U^{\dagger}(w')\big)U(y)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(w)\big(U(x')-U(w')\big)D^{h}\big(U^{\dagger}(y)-U^{\dagger}(w)\big)U(w)\Big\}\Big]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:S-I.II-3}
\end{align}
where: $$R^{i}(\mathbf{q},\mathbf{p})  = -2q^{i}\log\left(\frac{\mathbf{p}^{2}}{\mathbf{q}^{2}}\right)+\frac{(2\mathbf{p}^{2}-2\,\mathbf{q}\cdot\mathbf{p}+\mathbf{q}^{2})\,q^{i}+\mathbf{q}^{2}p^{i}}{(\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})^{2}}\,\log\left[\frac{\mathbf{q}^{2}}{(\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})^{2}}\right]$$
Two $\rho$ terms after symmetrisation
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.II}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(2\rho)}&=-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{64\pi^{6}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\int_{x,y,x'}\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\int d^{2}p\,d^{2}q\;e^{iq\cdot(w-y)}\,e^{ip\cdot(y-x)}\,\frac{(x'-w')^{i}}{(x'-w')^{2}\,\mathbf{q}^{2}}\,\frac{1}{2}\bigg[\frac{R^{i}(\mathbf{q},\mathbf{p})}{\mathbf{p}^{2}}-\frac{R^{i}(\mathbf{q},\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})}{(\mathbf{q}-\mathbf{p})^{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)\big(U(x)-U(w)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(x)-U^{\dagger}(w')\big)U(y)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(w)\big(U(y)-U(w)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(x)-U^{\dagger}(w')\big)U(w)\Big\}\Big]\notag\\
& +\Big(-i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)\big(U(x)-U(w)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(y)-U^{\dagger}(w')\big)U(y)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(w)\big(U(y)-U(w)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(y)-U^{\dagger}(w')\big)U(w)\Big\}\Big]\bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:S-I.II-2}
\end{align}
Genuine two-$\rho$ term:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.II}_{1}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(2\rho)} & = +\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}\,N_{c}}{32\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\Big(-\frac{67}{9}+\frac{\pi^{2}}{3}\Big)\int_{w,w',x,x'} e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\;\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\notag \\
& \times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[{\rm Tr}\Big\{\big(U^{\dagger}(x')-U^{\dagger}(w')\big)\big(U(w)-U(x)\big)\Big\}\Big]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:S-I.II-2o}
\end{align}
\paragraph*{Row III}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ part:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.III}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(3\rho)}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\int_{x,y,z,x'}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\notag\\
&+\frac{\arccos\left(\frac{(x-w)^{2}-(x-w)\cdot (x-z)}{\sqrt{(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w)^{2}}}\right)}{\sqrt{(x-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-\left[(x-w)\cdot (x-z)\right]^{2}}\,(z-w)^{2}}\,\bigg[\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(x-w)\notag\\
&-\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-z)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(x-z)\notag\\
&+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)-\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)\bigg]\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&+\bigg[-\frac{1}{2}\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}-\int_{0}^{1}d\xi\Big[\frac{1}{\xi}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot(z-w)\,\xi}\bigg]\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\notag\\
&+\int_{0}^{1}d\xi\Big[\frac{1}{\xi}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot(z-w)\,\xi}\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(+i\Big)\,\sigma(x',x,y)\Big[{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)\big(U(z)-U(y)\big)D^{h}\big(U^{\dagger}(x')-U^{\dagger}(w')\big)U(x)\Big\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{-\int_{0}^{1}d\xi\Big[\frac{1}{\xi}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot(z-w)\,\xi}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(+i\Big)\,\sigma(x',x,y)\Big[{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)\big(U(w)-U(y)\big)D^{h}\big(U^{\dagger}(x')-U^{\dagger}(w')\big)U(x)\Big\}\Big]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:S-I.III-3}
\end{align}
Two $\rho$ term after symmetrization
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.III}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(2\rho)}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\int_{x,y,z,x'}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\notag\\
&+\frac{\arccos\left(\frac{(x-w)^{2}-(x-w)\cdot (x-z)}{\sqrt{(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w)^{2}}}\right)}{\sqrt{(x-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-\left[(x-w)\cdot (x-z)\right]^{2}}\,(z-w)^{2}}\,\bigg[\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(x-w)\notag\\
&-\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-z)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(x-z)\notag\\
&+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)-\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x-z)}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot(z-w)\bigg]\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&+\bigg[-\frac{1}{2}\log\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}-\int_{0}^{1}d\xi\Big[\frac{1}{\xi}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot(z-w)\,\xi}\bigg]\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\notag\\
&+\int_{0}^{1}d\xi\Big[\frac{1}{\xi}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot(z-w)\,\xi}\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)\big(U(z)-U(y)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(x)-U^{\dagger}(w')\big)U(x)\Big\}\Big]\notag\\
& +\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)\big(U(z)-U(x)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(x')-U^{\dagger}(w')\big)U(x)\Big\}\Big]\notag\\
& +\Big(-\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)\big(U(z)-U(y)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(y)-U^{\dagger}(w')\big)U(x)\Big\}\Big]\bigg]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{-\int_{0}^{1}d\xi\Big[\frac{1}{\xi}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot(z-w)\,\xi}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)\big(U(w)-U(y)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(x)-U^{\dagger}(w')\big)U(x)\Big\}\Big]\notag\\
& +\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)\big(U(w)-U(x)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(x')-U^{\dagger}(w')\big)U(x)\Big\}\Big]\notag\\
& +\Big(-\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)\big(U(w)-U(y)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(y)-U^{\dagger}(w')\big)U(x)\Big\}\Big]\bigg]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:S-I.III-2}
\end{align}
\paragraph*{Row IV}
\textsc{NA} - canceled with Group III, Row I
\paragraph*{Row V}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ part:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.V}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(3\rho)}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{1}\frac{d\xi}{2\pi}\int_{x,z,y,y',x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot(z-w')}\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{e^{-ik\cdot(x-z)\,\xi}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\notag\\
&-\Big[\frac{1}{\xi}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot(x-z)\,\xi}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\notag\\
&-\Big[\frac{1}{\xi}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot(x-z)}e^{-ik\cdot(z-x)\,\xi}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(+2i\Big)\,\sigma(x',y,y')\Big[{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)\big(U(x)-U(y')\big)D^{h}\big(U^{\dagger}(x')-U^{\dagger}(w')\big)U(z)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad+{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(y')\big(U(x')-U(w')\big)D^{h}\big(U^{\dagger}(z)-U^{\dagger}(y)\big)U(y')\Big\}\Big]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:S-I.V-3}
\end{align}
Two $\rho$ term after symmetrization
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.V}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(2\rho)} &=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{1}\frac{d\xi}{2\pi}\int_{x,z,y,y',x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot(z-w')}\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{e^{-ik\cdot(x-z)\,\xi}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\notag\\
&-\Big[\frac{1}{\xi}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot(x-z)\,\xi}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\notag\\
&-\Big[\frac{1}{\xi}\Big]_{+}e^{-ik\cdot(x-z)}e^{-ik\cdot(z-x)\,\xi}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-x)\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,y')\Big[-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)\big(U(x)-U(y')\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(y)-U^{\dagger}(w')\big)U(z)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')\big(U(z)-U(y)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(y)-U^{\dagger}(w')\big)U(y')\Big\}\Big]\notag\\
& +\Big(-i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y')\,n(y',y)\Big[-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)\big(U(x)-U(y')\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(y')-U^{\dagger}(w')\big)U(z)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y')\big(U(z)-U(y)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(y')-U^{\dagger}(w')\big)U(y')\Big\}\Big]\bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:S-I.V-2}
\end{align}
Genuine two-$\rho$ terms:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm I.V}_{2}}{d^{3}k} & =\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{1}\frac{d\xi}{2\pi}\int_{x,y,z,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot(z-w')}\notag\\
&\times\Bigg\{\frac{e^{-ik\cdot(x-z)\,\xi}}{\xi\,(y-x)^{2}+(1-\xi)\,(y-z)^{2}}\bigg[\xi(1-\xi)\,\frac{(y-x)^{2}-(y-z)^{2}}{(x-z)^{2}}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\notag\\
&+(1-\xi)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\Big[(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)+\frac{1}{2}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)-\frac{(y-x)^{2}-(y-z)^{2}}{2\,(x-z)^{2}}\Big]\notag\\
&+\xi\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\Big[(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\frac{1}{2}(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)-\frac{(y-x)^{2}-(y-z)^{2}}{2\,(x-z)^{2}}\Big]\notag\\
&+\frac{(y-x)^{2}}{(y-z)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\,(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)\Big]\notag\\
&+\frac{(y-z)^{2}}{(y-x)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}-\frac{1}{2}\,(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\Big]\notag\\
&-(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')+\frac{1}{2}\,(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\notag\\
&-(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')-\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-x)\bigg]\notag\\
&{-\Big[\frac{1}{\xi}\Big]_{+}\frac{e^{-ik\cdot(x-z)\,\xi}}{(y-z)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\,(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)\Big]}\notag\\
&{-\Big[\frac{1}{1-\xi}\Big]_{+}\frac{e^{-ik\cdot(x-z)\,\xi}}{(y-x)^{2}}\Big[-\frac{(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')}{(x-z)^{2}}-\frac{1}{2}\,(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(z-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)\Big]}\Bigg\}\notag\\
&\times\frac{n(x',y)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)\big(U(x')-U(w')\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(z)-U^{\dagger}(x)\big)U(x)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\Big\{\big(U^{\dagger}(x')-U^{\dagger}(w')\big)\big(U(y)-U(x)\big)\Big\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}N_{c}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot(z-w')}\,e^{-ik\cdot(x-z)}\,\frac{11}{6}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-x)\,\frac{(y-x)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{(x-z)^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\frac{n(x',y)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[{\rm Tr}\Big\{\big(U^{\dagger}(x')-U^{\dagger}(w')\big)\big(U(y)-U(x)\big)\Big\}\Big]\,+{\rm c.c.}\,,\label{eq:S-I.V-2o}
\end{align}
\subsubsection{Group II}
\paragraph*{Row I}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ part:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(3\rho)}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,w,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{-\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(+2i\Big)\,\sigma(x',y,x)\Big[-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)\big(U(w')-U(x')\big)D^{h}\big(U^{\dagger}(x)-U^{\dagger}(z)\big)U(z)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad+{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)\big(U(z)-U(y)\big)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')\big(U(w)-U(z)\big)\Big\}-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)D^{h}\big(U^{\dagger}(z)-U^{\dagger}(y)\big)U(x')\Big\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w')^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w')\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w')^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(+2i\Big)\,\sigma(x,x',y)\Big[-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)\big(U(w)-U(x')\big)D^{h}\big(U^{\dagger}(x)-U^{\dagger}(z)\big)U(z)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad+{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)\big(U(z)-U(y)\big)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')\big(U(w')-U(z)\big)\Big\}-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)D^{h}\big(U^{\dagger}(z)-U^{\dagger}(y)\big)U(x')\Big\}\Big]\Bigg]\label{eq:S-II.I-3}
\end{align}
Two $\rho$ term after symmetrization:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(2\rho)} &=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,w,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{-\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\Big\{\big(U^{\dagger}(w')-U^{\dagger}(x')\big)\big(U(w)-U(z)\big)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(w')-U^{\dagger}(x')\big)U(z)\Big\}+{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)\big(U(z)-U(x)\big)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')\big(U(w)-U(z)\big)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(z)-U^{\dagger}(x)\big)U(x')\Big\}\Big]\notag\\
& +\Big(-i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\Big\{\big(U^{\dagger}(w')-U^{\dagger}(x)\big)\big(U(w)-U(z)\big)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(w')-U^{\dagger}(x)\big)U(z)\Big\}+{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)\big(U(z)-U(y)\big)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)\big(U(w)-U(z)\big)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(z)-U^{\dagger}(y)\big)U(x)\Big\}\Big]\bigg]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w')^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w')\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w')\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w')^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\Big\{\big(U^{\dagger}(w)-U^{\dagger}(x)\big)\big(U(w')-U(z)\big)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(w)-U^{\dagger}(x)\big)U(z)\Big\}+{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)\big(U(z)-U(y)\big)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)\big(U(w')-U(z)\big)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(z)-U^{\dagger}(y)\big)U(x)\Big\}\Big]\notag\\
& +\Big(-i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\Big\{\big(U^{\dagger}(w)-U^{\dagger}(x')\big)\big(U(w')-U(z)\big)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(w)-U^{\dagger}(x')\big)U(z)\Big\}+{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)\big(U(z)-U(x)\big)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')\big(U(w')-U(z)\big)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(z)-U^{\dagger}(x)\big)U(x')\Big\}\Big]\bigg]\Bigg]\label{eq:S-II.I-2}
\end{align}
Genuine two-$\rho$ terms:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2a}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{2\rho}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w^{\prime})}\,\frac{1}{\big[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\right]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\frac{1}{2}\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\notag \\ & +\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime}) -\frac{1}{4}\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\notag \\ & +(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})+\frac{1}{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{2}\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)-\frac{1}{4}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\Bigg]\notag\\ &\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\Big\{\big(U^{\dagger}(w')-U^{\dagger}(z)\big)\big(U(w)-U(z)\big)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\Big\{\big(U^{\dagger}(x')-U^{\dagger}(z)\big)\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)\Big\}-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(w')-U^{\dagger}(z)\big)U(z)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)\big(U(x')-U(z)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(w)-U^{\dagger}(z)\big)U(z)\Big\}\Big]\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2b}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(2\rho)}&= -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w^{\prime})}\,\frac{1}{\big[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-w)^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\right]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\frac{1}{2}\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\notag \\ & +\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime}) -\frac{1}{4}\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\notag \\ & +(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})+\frac{1}{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{2}\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)-\frac{1}{4}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\Bigg]\notag\\ &\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\Big\{\big(U^{\dagger}(w')-U^{\dagger}(z)\big)\big(U(w)-U(z)\big)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\Big\{\big(U^{\dagger}(x')-U^{\dagger}(z)\big)\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)\Big\}-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(w')-U^{\dagger}(z)\big)U(z)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)\big(U(x')-U(z)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(w)-U^{\dagger}(z)\big)U(z)\Big\}\Big]\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2c}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(2\rho)} &= \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w^{\prime})}\,\frac{1}{\big[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\right]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[-\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg[\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\,(z-w)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(z-w)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x-w)\cdot(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,(x-z)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})}{2\,(x-z)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)}{4\,(x-z)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\bigg]\notag\\
&-\frac{(x-w)^{2}}{(x-z)^{2}}\bigg[\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x-z)\,(z-w)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}-\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x-z)\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(z-w)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & -\frac{(x-w)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})\,(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)}{2\,(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)}{4\,(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\bigg]\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\{(x-z)^{2}\bigg[\frac{(x-w)\cdot(z-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{4\,(x-z)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag\\ &+\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(z-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\bigg]-(x-w)^{2}\bigg[\frac{(x-z)\cdot(z-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & -\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{4\,(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}+\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(z-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & -\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}-\frac{1}{2}\,\frac{(x-z)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}\Bigg]\notag\\ &\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\Big\{\big(U^{\dagger}(w')-U^{\dagger}(z)\big)\big(U(w)-U(z)\big)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\Big\{\big(U^{\dagger}(x')-U^{\dagger}(z)\big)\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)\Big\}-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(w')-U^{\dagger}(z)\big)U(z)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)\big(U(x')-U(z)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(w)-U^{\dagger}(z)\big)U(z)\Big\}\Big]\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2d}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(2\rho)}&= \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w^{\prime})}\,\frac{1}{\big[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\right]\notag\\
&\times\Bigg[\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}\bigg[(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})+\frac{1}{2}\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\notag \\ & +\frac{1}{2}\,(x-w)\cdot(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})+\frac{1}{4}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\,(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot(x-w)\bigg]\notag\\
&+\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\bigg[(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})-\frac{1}{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)+\frac{1}{4}\,(x-z)\cdot(x^{\prime}-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\bigg]\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}-(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}}\bigg\{(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\bigg[\frac{(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag\\ &+\frac{(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-z)\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w)^{2}}\bigg]\notag\\
&-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\bigg[\frac{(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}-\frac{(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}-\frac{(x^{\prime}-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{4\,(z-w)^{2}}\bigg]\bigg\}\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\,\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\,(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}\,\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}\,\big[(x^{\prime}-z)^{2}-(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}\big]}\Bigg]\notag\\ &\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\Big\{\big(U^{\dagger}(w')-U^{\dagger}(z)\big)\big(U(w)-U(z)\big)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\Big\{\big(U^{\dagger}(x')-U^{\dagger}(z)\big)\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)\Big\}-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(w')-U^{\dagger}(z)\big)U(z)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)\big(U(x')-U(z)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(w)-U^{\dagger}(z)\big)U(z)\Big\}\Big]\\
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.I}_{2e}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(2\rho)}&= -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x^{\prime}}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w^{\prime})}\,\frac{1}{(z-w)^{2}\,(z-w^{\prime})^{2}}\notag\\ &\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\Big\{\big(U^{\dagger}(w')-U^{\dagger}(z)\big)\big(U(w)-U(z)\big)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad+N_{c}{\rm Tr}\Big\{\big(U^{\dagger}(x')-U^{\dagger}(z)\big)\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)\Big\}-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(w')-U^{\dagger}(z)\big)U(z)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)\big(U(x')-U(z)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(w)-U^{\dagger}(z)\big)U(z)\Big\}\Big]\notag
\end{align}
\paragraph*{Row II}
\textsc{NA}
\paragraph*{Row III}
\textsc{NA}
\paragraph*{Row IV}

\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm II.IV}_{\rm fin}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(2\rho)}= &  +\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}\,N_{c}}{4\pi^{3}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{k^{+}/\vee}^{1}\frac{d\xi}{2\pi}\,\frac{1-\xi}{\xi}\int_{x,y,x',y'}e^{-ik\cdot(y-y')\frac{1}{\xi}}\Big[\mathcal{K}(x-y)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\notag \\ & -2\,\mathcal{K}(x-y)\cdot(y'-y)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-y)\Big]\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\,{\rm Tr}\Big\{\big(U^{\dagger}(y')-U^{\dagger}(x')\big)\big(U(y)-U(x)\big)\Big\}\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}\,N_{c}}{2\pi^{3}}\,\frac{1}{k^{2}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,x',z}\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\,{\rm Tr}\Big\{\big(U^{\dagger}(z)-U^{\dagger}(x')\big)\big(U(z)-U(x)\big)\Big\}\label{eq:S-II.IVrem}
\end{align}
\subsubsection{Group III}
\paragraph*{Row I}
\textsc{NA} - canceled with Group I, Row IV
\paragraph*{Row II}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ part:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.II}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(3\rho)}&=-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,x',z,w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(+i\Big)\,\sigma(y,x',x)\Big[-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(z)\big(U(z)-U(y)\big)D^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')\big(U(w)-U(z)\big)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad+{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x')\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)D^{h}\big(U^{\dagger}(z)-U^{\dagger}(y)\big)U(x')\Big\}\Big]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:S-III.II-3}
\end{align}
Two $\rho$ term after symmetrization
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.II}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(2\rho)}&=-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,x',z,w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-z)}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(y-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)\bigg]\log\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)\big(U(z)-U(y)\big)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)\big(U(w)-U(z)\big)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(z)-U^{\dagger}(y)\big)U(y)\Big\}\Big]\notag\\
& +\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)\big(U(z)-U(y)\big)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)\big(U(w)-U(z)\big)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(z)-U^{\dagger}(y)\big)U(x)\Big\}\Big]\notag\\
& +\Big(-\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)\big(U(z)-U(x)\big)T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')\big(U(w)-U(z)\big)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x')\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(z)-U^{\dagger}(x)\big)U(x')\Big\}\Big]\bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:S-III.II-2}
\end{align}
\paragraph*{Row III}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ part:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.III}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(3\rho)}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{d\xi}{2\pi}\int_{w,x',y',z,y,x}e^{-ik\cdot(w-y')}e^{-ik\cdot(z-y')\,\xi}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{1}{1+\xi}\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&-\Big[\frac{1}{\xi\,(1+\xi)}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(+2i\Big)\,\sigma(x',y,x)\Big[{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(w)\big(U(z)-U(x)\big)D^{h}\big(U^{\dagger}(y')-U^{\dagger}(x')\big)U(w)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad+{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)\big(U(y')-U(x')\big)D^{h}\big(U^{\dagger}(w)-U^{\dagger}(y)\big)U(x)\Big\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(+2i\Big)\,\sigma(x',y,x)\Big[{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(w)\big(U(z)-U(x)\big)D^{h}\big(U^{\dagger}(z)-U^{\dagger}(x')\big)U(w)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad+{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)\big(U(z)-U(x')\big)D^{h}\big(U^{\dagger}(w)-U^{\dagger}(y)\big)U(x)\Big\}\Big]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:S-III.III-3}
\end{align}
Two $\rho$ term after symmetrization:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.III}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(2\rho)}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{d\xi}{2\pi}\int_{w,x',y',z,y,x}e^{-ik\cdot(w-y')}e^{-ik\cdot(z-y')\,\xi}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{1}{1+\xi}\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&-\Big[\frac{1}{\xi\,(1+\xi)}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(w)\big(U(z)-U(x)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(y')-U^{\dagger}(y)\big)U(w)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)\big(U(w)-U(y)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(y')-U^{\dagger}(y)\big)U(x)\Big\}\Big]\notag\\
& +\Big(-i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(w)\big(U(z)-U(x)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(y')-U^{\dagger}(x)\big)U(w)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)\big(U(w)-U(y)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(y')-U^{\dagger}(x)\big)U(x)\Big\}\Big]\bigg]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(w)\big(U(z)-U(x)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(z)-U^{\dagger}(y)\big)U(w)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)\big(U(w)-U(y)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(z)-U^{\dagger}(y)\big)U(x)\Big\}\Big]\notag\\
& +\Big(-i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(w)\big(U(z)-U(x)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(z)-U^{\dagger}(x)\big)U(w)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)\big(U(w)-U(y)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(z)-U^{\dagger}(x)\big)U(x)\Big\}\Big]\bigg]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:S-III.III-2}
\end{align}
Genuine two-$\rho$ terms:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.III}_{2}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(2\rho)} & =\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{d\xi}{2\pi}\int_{w,x',y',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot(w-y')}e^{-ik\cdot(z-y')\,\xi}\,\frac{1}{\xi\,(x-z)^{2}+(x-w)^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\Bigg\{\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg[\frac{\xi\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}{(z-w)^{2}\,(1+\xi)^{2}}\notag\\
&+\frac{\xi}{1+\xi}\Big((x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-w)-\frac{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\Big)\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{1+\xi}\Big((x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(z-w)+\frac{1}{2}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\frac{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\Big)\bigg]\notag\\
&+\frac{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}{(1+\xi)\,(x-w)^{2}}\Big[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)+\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\Big]\notag\\
&-\Big[(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\notag\\
&+(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\notag\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\Big]\Bigg\}\notag\\
&\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\Big\{\big(U^{\dagger}(y')-U^{\dagger}(x')\big)\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)\big(U(w)-U(z)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(y')-U^{\dagger}(x')\big)U(z)\Big\}\Big]\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{d\xi}{2\pi}\Big[\frac{1}{\xi\,(1+\xi)}\Big]_{+}\int_{w,x',y',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot(w-y')}e^{-ik\cdot(z-y')\,\xi}\,\frac{1}{(x-w)^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\Big[(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)+\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\,(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\Big]\notag\\
&\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\Big\{\big(U^{\dagger}(y')-U^{\dagger}(x')\big)\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)\big(U(w)-U(z)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(y')-U^{\dagger}(x')\big)U(z)\Big\}\Big]\notag\\
&-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{d\xi}{2\pi}\int_{w,x',y',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot(w-y')}e^{-ik\cdot(z-y')\,\xi}\,\frac{\xi}{\xi\,(x-z)^{2}+(x-w)^{2}}\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\Big[(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\Big]\notag\\
&\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\Big\{\big(U^{\dagger}(y')-U^{\dagger}(x')\big)\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)\big(U(w)-U(z)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(y')-U^{\dagger}(x')\big)U(z)\Big\}\Big]\notag\\
&-\Big[(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)-\frac{1}{2}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\Big]\notag\\
&\times\frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\Big\{\big(U^{\dagger}(z)-U^{\dagger}(x')\big)\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)\big(U(w)-U(z)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(z)-U^{\dagger}(x')\big)U(z)\Big\}\Big]\bigg\}\notag\\
& +\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{w,x',w',z,x}e^{-ik\cdot(w-w')}\,\frac{(x-w)^{2}}{\big[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\big]}\,\log\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\right]\notag \\ &  \bigg[\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(z-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)-\frac{1}{2}\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)\bigg]   \times\notag \\ & \frac{n(x',x)}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big[N_{c}{\rm Tr}\Big\{\big(U^{\dagger}(z)-U^{\dagger}(x')\big)\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)\Big\}\notag\\&\qquad-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(z)\big(U(w)-U(z)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(z)-U^{\dagger}(x')\big)U(z)\Big\}\Big]\,+{\rm c.c.}\,,\label{eq:S-III.III-2o}
\end{align}
\paragraph*{Row IV}
\textsc{NA}
\paragraph*{Row V}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ part:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.V}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(3\rho)} &=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{d\xi}{2\pi}\int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-y')}e^{-ik\cdot(z-y')\,\xi}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{1}{1+\xi}\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&-\Big[\frac{1}{\xi\,(1+\xi)}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(+i\Big)\,\sigma(x',x,y)\Big[{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)\big(U(y')-U(x')\big)D^{h}\big(U^{\dagger}(y)-U^{\dagger}(w)\big)U(x)\Big\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(+i\Big)\,\sigma(x',x,y)\Big[{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(x)\big(U(z)-U(x')\big)D^{h}\big(U^{\dagger}(y)-U^{\dagger}(w)\big)U(x)\Big\}\Big]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:S-III.V-3}
\end{align}
Two $\rho$ terms after symmetrization: 
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.V}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(2\rho)}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{d\xi}{2\pi}\int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-y')}e^{-ik\cdot(z-y')\,\xi}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{1}{1+\xi}\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&-\Big[\frac{1}{\xi\,(1+\xi)}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)\big(U(y)-U(w)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(y')-U^{\dagger}(x)\big)U(x)\Big\}\Big]\notag\\
& +\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)\big(U(x)-U(w)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(y')-U^{\dagger}(x')\big)U(x)\Big\}\Big]\notag\\
& +\Big(-\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)\big(U(y)-U(w)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(y')-U^{\dagger}(y)\big)U(x)\Big\}\Big]\bigg]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)\big(U(y)-U(w)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(z)-U^{\dagger}(x)\big)U(x)\Big\}\Big]\notag\\
& +\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)\big(U(x)-U(w)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(z)-U^{\dagger}(x')\big)U(x)\Big\}\Big]\notag\\
& +\Big(-\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)\big(U(y)-U(w)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(z)-U^{\dagger}(y)\big)U(x)\Big\}\Big]\bigg]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:S-III.V-2}
\end{align}
\paragraph*{Row VI}
Symmetrised three-$\rho$ part:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.VI}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(3\rho)}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{d\xi}{2\pi}\int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-y')}e^{-ik\cdot(z-y')\,\xi}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{1}{1+\xi}\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&-\Big[\frac{1}{\xi\,(1+\xi)}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(+i\Big)\,\sigma(x',y,x)\Big[{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(y)\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)D^{h}\big(U^{\dagger}(y')-U^{\dagger}(x')\big)U(y)\Big\}\Big]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{N_{c}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,\Big(+i\Big)\,\sigma(x',y,x)\Big[{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{h}U^{\dagger}(y)\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)D^{h}\big(U^{\dagger}(z)-U^{\dagger}(x')\big)U(y)\Big\}\Big]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:S-III.VI-3}
\end{align}
Two $\rho$ term after symmetrization: 
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N^{\rm III.VI}}{d^{3}k}\bigg|_{(2\rho)}&=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{4\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{d\xi}{2\pi}\int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}e^{-ik\cdot(w-y')}e^{-ik\cdot(z-y')\,\xi}\Bigg[\notag\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{1}{1+\xi}\Big[\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\,\mathcal{K}(y-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\Big]\notag\\
&-\Big[\frac{1}{\xi\,(1+\xi)}\Big]_{+}\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x-z)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\notag\\
&-\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-y')\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(y')-U^{\dagger}(y)\big)U(y)\Big\}\Big]\notag\\
& +\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(y')-U^{\dagger}(x')\big)U(x)\Big\}\Big]\notag\\
& +\Big(-\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(y')-U^{\dagger}(x)\big)U(y)\Big\}\Big]\bigg]\notag\\
&+\bigg\{+\mathcal{K}(y'-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(y-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\bigg\}\notag\\
&\times \frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}-1}\bigg[\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-y)\,n(y,x)\Big[-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(z)-U^{\dagger}(y)\big)U(y)\Big\}\Big]\notag\\
& +\Big(+\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(y-x)\,n(x,x')\Big[-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(x)\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(z)-U^{\dagger}(x')\big)U(x)\Big\}\Big]\notag\\
& +\Big(-\frac{1}{2}i\Big)\delta^{(2)}(x'-x)\,n(x,y)\Big[-{\rm Tr}\Big\{T^{e}U^{\dagger}(y)\big(U(x)-U(z)\big)T^{e}\big(U^{\dagger}(z)-U^{\dagger}(x)\big)U(y)\Big\}\Big]\bigg]\Bigg]+{\rm c.c.}\label{eq:S-III.VI-2}
\end{align}

\subsection{Checks}\label{sec:F3}
\noindent All checks with \texttt{python3 -I}, random adjoint Wilson lines $U=2{\rm tr}(t^{a}Vt^{b}V^{\dagger})$ of $SU(3)$ and $SU(4)$ (Haar-random $V$, independent at every point), relative deviations (scripts in \texttt{scripts/}, logs \texttt{*.log}). Every check evaluates the colour factors \emph{as printed}: the \LaTeX\ source of this file is parsed (\texttt{s16\_lib.py}: index tensors $U^{ab}(x)$, $f^{abc}$, $d^{abc}$, $\delta^{ab}$, $N_{c}$; traces with $T^{e}$, $D^{h}$, $U$, $U^{\dagger}$ and differences) and compared with the colour tensors of our verified file.
\begin{enumerate}
\item \textbf{Factorised $\rho$-explicit colour structures} (\texttt{s16c\_sec8\_colour.py}, \texttt{s16e\_final\_checks.py}): the 13 three-$\rho$ tensors $C^{abc}$ (Group I Rows II, III ($z$, $w$), V; Group II Row I (two); Group III Rows II, III, V, VI (each with $y'$ and $z$)) and the 8 two-$\rho$ colour factors (Group I Rows II, V and its $\frac{11}{6}$ line; Group II Row I second part, Row IV and its remnant; Group III Row III second part with $y'$ and $z$) equal the original ones of Sec.~6 to $\le1.6\times10^{-15}$ (162 colour blocks of Sec.~\ref{sec:F1} read from this file, plus the five equations of Group II Row I second part and the factors $L(v)\times R$ of Group III Row III second part, $\le1.8\times10^{-15}$). Control: one sign changed in the Group II Row I tensor gives $1.95$.
\item \textbf{Singlet forms} of Sec.~\ref{sec:F2}: every printed coefficient times trace bracket (61 items: 13 three-$\rho$, 33 contact, 15 genuine two-$\rho$) equals the average of the corresponding colour factor of Sec.~\ref{sec:F1} computed by direct contraction ($t\,C^{abc}d^{abc}$; $g\,C^{abc}f^{abc}$ with the Wilson lines at the coinciding charges; $\delta^{b'b}$ contraction): largest relative deviation $7\times10^{-14}$ (122 items $\times$ 3 configurations). The trace identities $f^{ijk}d^{lmn}O^{il}B^{jm}C^{kn}=i{\rm Tr}\{T^{h}O^{\dagger}CD^{h}B^{\dagger}O\}$ and $f^{ijk}f^{lmn}O^{il}B^{jm}C^{kn}={\rm Tr}\{T^{e}O^{\dagger}CT^{e}B^{\dagger}O\}$ for arbitrary $B$, $C$ and orthogonal $O$: $2.6\times10^{-15}$.
\item \textbf{Longitudinal integrals in $\xi$} (\texttt{s16d\_xi.py}, quadrature, random $k^{+}$, recoil arguments and complex transverse coefficients, plus prescriptions implemented by their definitions): Group I Row III $1.6\times10^{-16}$; Group I Row V three-$\rho$ $1.1\times10^{-16}$, two-$\rho$ $1.0\times10^{-15}$; Group II Row IV with $\xi=\frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}+k^{+}}$ (finite $\vee=4k^{+}$, $12k^{+}$; Jacobian $k^{+}/\xi^{2}$, limits $1\to k^{+}/\vee$) $1.3\times10^{-16}$, integrand pointwise $7\times10^{-15}$; Group III Rows III, V, VI and Row III second part (convergence factor $e^{-\epsilon\xi}=e^{-\epsilon p^{+}/k^{+}}$, $\epsilon=0.5,0.05$; the change of variables is exact for every $\epsilon$) $\le1.2\times10^{-12}$; at $\epsilon=0$ (Fourier quadrature) $\int_{0}^{\infty}d\xi\frac{e^{-is\xi}}{1+\xi}=e^{is}E_{1}(is)$ and $\int_{0}^{\infty}d\xi\big[\frac{1}{\xi(1+\xi)}\big]_{+}e^{-is\xi}=-\gamma_{E}-\log|s|-\frac{i\pi}{2}{\rm sgn}(s)-e^{is}E_{1}(is)$ to $5\times10^{-11}$. Control: the prefactor $\frac{1}{k^{+\,2}}$ kept after $dp^{+}\to d\xi$ (missing Jacobian) gives $0.47$. No $p^{+}$ is left in the equations of Secs.~\ref{sec:F1}, \ref{sec:F2}; the transformations of the kernels are string replacements with asserted counts (\texttt{make\_fixed2.py}), each of which is one of the identities checked here.
\item \textbf{Operator level} (\texttt{s16e}): the symmetrisation identities \eqref{eq:D-ord} (as corrected), \eqref{eq:D-r12}, \eqref{eq:D-r21} with $SU(3)$ charges $\rho^{a}=t^{a}$ on one to three sites (all coincidence patterns) and a random $C^{abc}$: $2.6\times10^{-16}$, $3.9\times10^{-16}$, $3.6\times10^{-16}$. The missing brackets of Eq.~\eqref{eq:D-II.I-3}: as typeset the colour tensor differs by $0.32$ (with the red braces $3\times10^{-16}$).
\item \textbf{The author's Sec.~7} (\texttt{s16b\_author2\_sec7.py}): all 61 colour items as printed equal the average of the Sec.~6 colour tensors (largest deviation $8\times10^{-15}$); Secs.~5--7 against the previous version and our verified file at the \TeX\ level: \texttt{s16a\_texcompare.py}.
\end{enumerate}
